**Strengths.** This is a complete, two-tier implementation: per-subject (or per-IP for public forms) scoping, request fingerprinting that also covers multipart uploads, durable storage that survives a Redis flush, a concurrency lock so duplicates in flight replay instead of double-executing, the documented 422 mismatch and replay header, and clients that generate and preserve keys across auth retries — all verified by tests I ran.

**Gaps.** Expired rows in idempotency_keys are never deleted, so the table grows forever on a busy builder; keys of any shape (even 1 character) are accepted despite the contract's min-8 schema. Only the JSON body and status are replayed, so a replayed create loses response headers such as Location or ETag. Scope for unauthenticated callers is the X-Forwarded-For hop, which TRUST_PROXY=true honours by default (lib/rateLimit.ts:32-38), so when the API is exposed without a proxy a client can choose its own scope. PATCH/PUT are outside the mechanism, including the PUT /booking-drafts upsert.

**Second auditor.** The mechanism is as described and I re-ran idempotency.test.ts (4 tests) green on kkt_audit_F2: scope = subjectId ?? clientIp (plugins/idempotency.ts:75), SHA-256 over method+url+fingerprint, Redis fast path + Postgres durable row with unique (scope_key,key), 2xx-only storage, Redis NX lock with 10 s wait and IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS fallback, multipart fingerprint hook, ERP client generating and preserving keys across refresh (apps/erp/src/lib/__tests__/api.test.ts:70-93). However the operational gap is confirmed and more serious than a minor note: idempotency_keys has an expires_at index (packages/db/src/schema/identity.ts:188) but grep of src/jobs and src/modules finds no purge, and every ERP POST writes a row, so the table grows without bound on a busy builder. Also confirmed: the plugin is POST-only (idempotency.ts:68), IdempotencyKeySchema (min 8) is defined in contracts but referenced nowhere in apps/api, only the JSON body/status is replayed (headers lost), and TRUST_PROXY defaults to 'true' (config.ts:44) so an unproxied deployment lets a public-form caller pick its own IP scope via X-Forwarded-For. These together justify a modest reduction from the first auditor's score.

**World-best would.** Add a nightly purge job and key-format validation, persist and replay the full response (status, headers, body) with a 'fingerprint' that includes the Content-Type, document the semantics in the OpenAPI header parameters, and make scope derivation refuse X-Forwarded-For unless the request actually arrived from the configured proxy CIDR.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/plugins/idempotency.ts:65-98 — preHandler scoped by req.subjectId ?? req.clientIp + key (line 75), SHA-256 over method+url+body (37-39), replay with Idempotent-Replayed: true (59-63), 422 IDEMPOTENCY_MISMATCH on a different fingerprint (60), 400 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED for required routes (71)`, `idempotency.ts:12,107-110 — 24 h TTL in Redis (fast path) and Postgres idempotency_keys (durable, unique (scope_key,key) per packages/db/src/schema/identity.ts:188); only 2xx responses stored (104); Redis lock with 10 s wait so concurrent duplicates replay the winner (82-96)`, `43 routes idempotent: true + POST /payments idempotent: "required" (modules/money/routes/payments.ts:38); public forms idempotent by IP (modules/web/publicForms.ts:91,128); multipart fingerprint via setIdempotencyFingerprint (idempotency.ts:25-35, lib/multipart.ts)`, `apps/api/test/integration/idempotency.test.ts (4 tests, passed on kkt_audit_F): replay after Redis flush via the Postgres record, mismatch 422, per-subject scoping, required-key 400; acceptance 06-cheques-reversal-idempotency.test.ts:130-158`, `apps/erp/src/lib/api.ts:419 — every POST carries a crypto.randomUUID() key; api.test.ts:70-95 proves the same key is reused on the retry after a token refresh; portal client.ts header comment and send() do the same`, `No purge of expired idempotency_keys rows anywhere (grep of src/jobs and modules/admin finds none; only an index on expires_at); IdempotencyKeySchema (contracts/common.ts:154, min 8) is not applied — the plugin only rejects keys > 128 chars (idempotency.ts:74)`

### F4. Optimistic concurrency semantics — 770/1000 (v1: 560)

**Strengths.** The core pattern is right: a single conditional-UPDATE helper used by 61 write paths, a conflict payload that names who changed the row and when, If-Match or body.version accepted interchangeably, and an ERP that sends the version on every edit and surfaces conflicts in a reload dialog; contracts carry version on every AuditedRow so clients always have it.

**Gaps.** The 'every mutable row' claim in ARCHITECTURE §3.9 is not true: nine mutable child tables lack a version and co-applicant edits are last-writer-wins. Sixteen state-change routes make the version optional, so a stale deactivate or photo edit overwrites silently. The If-Match value is not the ETag the API itself emits, which breaks standard HTTP conditional-request tooling and leaves a weak/strong-validator mismatch. changed_by depends on the audit log containing a row for the entity type passed; callers that omit entityType scan audit_logs by entity_id only.

**Second auditor.** Verified the core: lib/version.ts applyUpdate is a conditional UPDATE … WHERE version = expected with a 409 that names changed_by/changed_at from audit_logs; usage counts are 61 applyUpdate / 45 expectedVersion / 16 optionalExpectedVersion; conventions.test.ts:155-167 proves stale If-Match → 409 with details.current, and the ERP routes VERSION_CONFLICT to a dialog (conflict-store.ts:3-60). The ARCHITECTURE.md:52 claim 'Every mutable row has version; PATCH requires If-Match' is false: _common.ts:24 explicitly defines a timestamps() helper for 'rows that change but carry no optimistic-concurrency version' and ten tables use it (booking_drafts, booking_other_charges, co_applicants, rate_card_rates, rate_card_plc_rules, lead_follow_ups, enquiries, payment_plan_installments, demands, documents-era legacy), and PATCH /customers/:id/co-applicants/:caId (crm/customers.ts:266-274) is an unconditional last-writer-wins update of KYC-adjacent data. Sixteen routes make the version optional so a stale deactivate/photo edit wins silently, and If-Match carries a bare version while the ETag the server emits is a payload hash, so RFC 7232 conditional requests cannot round-trip. I lower slightly because the doc claim is contradicted by the code and the LWW path touches customer co-applicant data that the SPEC treats as legally significant.

**World-best would.** Give every table that any PATCH touches a version (or version the parent and bump it on child edits), make If-Match mandatory on all state changes, emit ETag: "<version>" on single-resource GETs so If-Match/ETag round-trip per RFC 7232, and include the conflicting field diff in the 409 payload so clients can merge instead of reload.

Evidence: `packages/db/src/schema/_common.ts:29-34 — mutable() adds version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1; 46 tables use it; documents.version added by drizzle/0001_central_fixes.sql (schema/documents.ts:32)`, `apps/api/src/lib/version.ts:13-22 expectedVersion (body.version or If-Match "<n>", 400 if neither), :61-69 applyUpdate = UPDATE … WHERE id AND version = expected RETURNING, :46-55 409 VERSION_CONFLICT with details.current, changed_by, changed_at read from the latest audit row; 45 expectedVersion + 16 optionalExpectedVersion + 61 applyUpdate call sites across modules`, `Ten mutable tables have no version column (timestamps() helper): booking_drafts, booking_other_charges, co_applicants, rate_card_rates, rate_card_plc_rules, lead_follow_ups, enquiries, payment_plan_installments, demands; PATCH /customers/:id/co-applicants/:caId (modules/crm/customers.ts:266-274) updates with no version check at all`, `If-Match is a bare version number while ETag (@fastify/etag, app.ts:73) is a payload hash: expectedVersion's Number(...) of a real ETag yields NaN → 400 (version.ts:17-21); the two headers do not form an RFC 7232 pair`, `Optional-version paths: masters deactivate/activate (masters/factory.ts:177), construction photos (construction/routes.ts:81), portal profile (portal/profile.ts:106), plan assignments (money/routes/plans.ts:115,140) silently fall back to the current version when no If-Match is sent`, `apps/erp/src/lib/api.ts:420 If-Match from the version option (108 call sites pass {version}); queries.ts:226-233 routes VERSION_CONFLICT to a dialog showing changed_by/changed_at (conflict-store.ts:46-60); conventions.test.ts:155-167 verifies stale If-Match → 409 with details.current`

### F5. List capabilities: filtering/sorting/search/CSV everywhere, allow-listed sort fields, strict query params — 800/1000 (v1: 610)

**Strengths.** A single ListQuery contract plus one paginate() helper gives every list endpoint the same offset and keyset modes, escaped search, allow-listed sorts, strict unknown-key rejection, planner-estimated counts for big tables and a streaming exact CSV, all verified by a 60-row integration fixture that walks cursors in both directions on tied keys.

**Gaps.** Search is `ILIKE %term%` over a few columns with no trigram/FTS index, which sequential-scans at a large builder's row counts; sort is single-column only; offset paging allows arbitrary deep pages. CSV is synchronous and silently truncated at 10 000 rows with no asynchronous export job despite ARCHITECTURE §6 saying 'CSV > 10k rows' never runs in a request. Dues, roznamcha, plan assignments, notifications and presets have no CSV at all, and date-range filters are per-module rather than part of the common contract.

**Second auditor.** Strong core, verified: ListQuerySchema (contracts/common.ts:192-203) with strict unknown-key rejection via routes.ts:71-74, allow-listed resolveSort (list.ts:110-115), escaped ILIKE, keyset cursors, planner-estimated totals, and the 60-row conventions fixture passed on my database. But two confirmed weaknesses matter for a large builder: (1) search is ILIKE '%term%' with no pg_trgm/tsvector index anywhere in packages/db (grep: 0 hits), so customer/lead/booking search sequential-scans at scale; (2) format=csv is synchronous, capped at CSV_EXPORT_MAX_ROWS = 10 000 (list.ts:242-247) with no truncation header, warning row or async job — ARCHITECTURE.md:106 says 'CSV > 10k rows … never runs in a request', but jobs/index.ts has no export job at all, so a finance user exporting a ledger or dues list above 10 000 rows receives a silently incomplete file. Dues, roznamcha, notifications and report presets have no CSV (0 occurrences of 'csv' in those route files). The silent truncation of financial exports is the reason for the extra reduction.

**World-best would.** Back search with pg_trgm/tsvector indexes and a ranked search term parser, support multi-key sort and saved filter presets everywhere, route exports above the cap to a BullMQ job with a signed download link, and generate the filter/sort allow-lists into the OpenAPI document as enums so clients cannot request an unsupported sort.

Evidence: `packages/contracts/src/common.ts:192-203 ListQuerySchema: page/page_size≤200, cursor+limit, search≤100, sort field|-field, include_inactive, format=csv, exact; 32 filter schemas extend it with typed filters (e.g. GET /customers exposes kyc_status, relationship_manager_id, has_active_booking, portal_enabled, phone per the OpenAPI dump)`, `apps/api/src/lib/routes.ts:71-74 strictQuery() makes every ZodObject querystring .strict(); conventions.test.ts:71-77 proves bogus=1, page_size=201, page=0, sort=password_hash, limit=0 → 400 VALIDATION_ERROR`, `apps/api/src/lib/list.ts:110-115 resolveSort against an allow-list (400 with the allowed names), :68-79 escaped ILIKE search, :57-59,172-199 keyset cursors on a microsecond-exact timestamp key with id tiebreak, :203-215 planner-estimated totals above 10 000 rows unless exact=true; 37 sort.allowed + 23 master sortable maps across modules`, `apps/api/src/lib/csv.ts:58-68 streaming RFC 4180 CSV with attachment header; list.ts:249-255 CSV = whole filtered list capped at CSV_EXPORT_MAX_ROWS = 10 000, exact count; 25 sendCsv sites plus the masters factory (factory.ts:114) covering 17 kinds; conventions.test.ts:140-152 verifies whole-list filtered CSV`, `Lists without CSV: money/routes/dues.ts, roznamcha.ts, plans.ts, notifications/index.ts, reports/routes/presets.ts and every portal/public list (grep of files lacking sendCsv)`, `apps/erp/src/lib/api.ts:539-546 api.list()/exportCsv() map the same query shape; apps/api/test/unit/list.test.ts covers escapeLike/resolveSort`

### F6. Error codes machine-readability & consistency — 705/1000 (v1: 650)

**Strengths.** The envelope is uniform across every failure path including framework errors, validation issues are structured per field, request ids are echoed, and the domain-specific 409/422 payloads (bucket cap, version conflict, missing stages, in-use counts) are typed in the shared contracts package with a status map and tests.

**Gaps.** The code registry is incomplete: 19 raised codes plus CONFLICT/ERROR fallbacks are absent, so the contracts' own type guard rejects real responses and the portal shows 'Something went wrong' instead of the server's message for closed tickets. BUSINESS_RULE hides ~55 rule identifiers behind one code with untyped free-text names, so clients cannot switch on them safely. There is no test that every code raised in apps/api exists in ERROR_CODES, no OpenAPI error documentation, and 422 is used both for business rules and idempotency mismatch without a shared details shape.

**Second auditor.** Confirmed by diffing every code raised in apps/api against ERROR_CODES: 19 raised codes are unregistered (ALREADY_DEMANDED, ALREADY_RATED, DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER, DUPLICATE_LEAD, DUPLICATE_PRESET, HAS_ACTIVE_BOOKING, INSUFFICIENT_CASH, JOB_ALREADY_RUNNING, NOT_IMPLEMENTED, PARKING_NOT_AVAILABLE, PAYMENT_NOT_CLEARED, PAYMENT_NOT_PENDING, PUBLISH_REQUIREMENTS, REGENERATE_BLOCKED, REOPEN_WINDOW_CLOSED, SUPER_ADMIN_ONLY, TICKET_CLOSED, UNDO_NOT_LATEST, UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA) plus the CONFLICT/ERROR fallbacks in errors.ts:38-41,89. Because ApiErrorSchema.code is z.enum(ERROR_CODES) and the portal client gates on isApiError (portal/src/api/client.ts:241-251), a customer who messages a closed ticket (service/ticketService.ts:456 TICKET_CLOSED) sees 'Something went wrong. Please try again.' with code UNKNOWN instead of the server's explanation — a real user-facing bug, not a cosmetic one. The contracts tests (adversarial.test.ts:352, registry.test.ts:94-98) only check a hand-written subset, so nothing would catch this. The envelope itself, structured validation issues and typed details for the registered 409s are good. Slightly lower than the first auditor because the breakage is on the customer-facing surface and there is no test guarding the registry.

**World-best would.** Make ApiError constructors accept only ErrorCode/RuleCode literals from contracts, enumerate every rule, add a unit test that greps raised codes against the registry, document each code's status and details schema in OpenAPI, and ship an i18n message table keyed by code so every client renders consistent text.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/lib/errors.ts:59-91 — every failure becomes {error:{code,message,details,request_id}}; Zod validation → 400 VALIDATION_ERROR with details.issues[{path,message,code}] (46-56,68); 503 keeps Retry-After; unknown routes get the same envelope (94-96)`, `packages/contracts/src/errors.ts:4-49 ERROR_CODES (44 codes) + ERROR_STATUS map + typed details schemas (VersionConflictDetails, InUseDetails, PlanStagesMissingDetails, BucketCapDetails, BusinessRuleDetails, ValidationErrorDetails); ApiErrorSchema.code is the enum (150-157) and isApiError rejects unknown codes (test/registry.test.ts:98)`, `19 codes raised by the API are missing from the registry (grep of ApiError.conflict/new ApiError vs ERROR_CODES): REGENERATE_BLOCKED, UNDO_NOT_LATEST, TICKET_CLOSED, PAYMENT_NOT_PENDING, PAYMENT_NOT_CLEARED, REOPEN_WINDOW_CLOSED, ALREADY_RATED, DUPLICATE_LEAD, DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER, SUPER_ADMIN_ONLY, UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA …; errors.ts:38-41,89 also emits CONFLICT and ERROR, which are not registered either`, `apps/portal/src/api/client.ts:241-251 — the portal relies on contracts.isApiError, so an unregistered code such as TICKET_CLOSED (raised in modules/portal) degrades to code UNKNOWN with a generic fallback message; the ERP client (apps/erp/src/lib/api.ts:84-90) and web client parse loosely and are unaffected`, `97 ApiError.rule() sites use ~55 free-form details.rule strings (STAGES_STARTED, PROJECT_INACTIVE, LEAD_CLOSED, KYC_STATUS_INVALID …) that are not enumerated or typed anywhere in contracts`, `docs/API-CONVENTIONS.md:17-18 status map documents ~20 codes; no generated list of all codes/rules`

### F7. API versioning & backward compatibility strategy — 600/1000 (v1: 490)

**Strengths.** A single, explicit /api/v1 prefix, one shared contracts package that makes any breaking change a compile error in all three front doors, response serialisers that tolerate additive fields, and fixed, documented surfaces for auth, public and portal give a clean starting point with no legacy debt to carry.

**Gaps.** There is no written compatibility strategy: no rule for additive-only changes, no deprecation headers (Deprecation/Sunset), no 410 path for retired routes, no dual-field or alias mechanism, and no v2 plan. Strict request schemas mean an older server rejects a newer client's extra field, and the portal's typed ErrorCode union breaks (code → UNKNOWN) when the server adds a code — exactly the native-app skew that an app-store-distributed portal will face. Nothing tells an outdated phone app to update.

**Second auditor.** Confirmed: grep for deprecat|sunset|/api/v2|min_version|x-api-version across apps/api/src, packages/contracts/src and docs/API-CONVENTIONS.md returns nothing; every module is mounted under /api/v1 (app.ts:108-120); portal/API have no minimum-client-version or capability negotiation. Mitigations are real but structural rather than a strategy: one shared contracts package makes breaking changes compile errors for the web clients, and Zod response serialisation tolerates additive fields. The decisive risk is the app-store-distributed portal: its ApiError.code type is the ErrorCode union, request schemas are strictObject, and there is no way to tell an outdated phone app to update, so skew will manifest as 400s and 'UNKNOWN' errors. I take 10 points off because the portal's enum-gated error parsing (verified above) makes even additive server changes (a new error code) client-breaking today, which is slightly worse than the first auditor's framing.

**World-best would.** Publish a compatibility contract (additive-only within v1, deprecation via Deprecation/Sunset headers and 410 after a dated window), add a minimum-supported-client check on /portal/auth/me, keep schema-diff tests that fail CI on breaking changes to contracts, and version the OpenAPI document with changelog entries per release.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/app.ts:108-120 — every module mounted under /api/v1; portal client (apps/portal/src/api/client.ts:54) and ERP client (apps/erp/src/lib/api.ts:247-250) hard-code /api/v1`, `grep for 410, deprecat, sunset, x-api-version, /api/v2 across apps/api/src and packages/contracts/src: no hits; docs/API-CONVENTIONS.md has no compatibility or deprecation policy (only the base path and fixed appendices)`, `packages/contracts is a workspace:* dependency of all four apps (apps/*/package.json), so server and web clients ship in lockstep; the native portal app (Expo, docs/STATUS.md 'native iOS/Android builds were not run') has no minimum-client-version or capability signal — grep for min_version/app_version in api/portal finds only the backup manifest (modules/admin/backup.ts:70)`, `Response schemas are compiled by fastify-type-provider-zod so unknown fields are stripped and additive fields are safe; request schemas are strictObject (179 uses in contracts) so adding a client field before the server is a 400`, `Health reports version (modules/system/index.ts:30, contracts HealthResponseSchema) but nothing negotiates it`, `apps/api/src/app.ts:108-120 — all modules registered with prefix "/api/v1"; no other prefix exists`

### F8. Testing: unit vs integration coverage, independence from live server/seed — 815/1000 (v1: 380)

**Strengths.** Tests are hermetic and deterministic: every file owns a migrated, truncated database and flushed Redis, drives the real Fastify app in-process, and the 13 acceptance files reproduce the owner's scenarios with line-level SPEC citations; pure engines and wire contracts have dense unit suites that I verified green, and adversarial integration files probe scope, privacy and limits.

**Gaps.** Coverage is never measured, so the 439/55 counts cannot be related to the 24.4k-line API; the ERP's 94 unit tests are outside CI and web/portal have none; API-level unit tests are 24 for a 20-module backend, leaving most handler logic covered only through slow serial integration runs on one database. Browser QA lives in markdown reports rather than a repeatable CI suite, and scenario 14 therefore cannot regress-fail a build. Doc counts (30 vs 31 files) have already drifted.

**Second auditor.** Verified the harness is hermetic: test/integration/helpers.ts pins TEST_DATABASE_URL/TEST_REDIS_URL, migrates and truncates its own database, and drives Fastify via inject; vitest.config.ts isolates to kkt_api_test / Redis db 1; conventions (7) and idempotency (4) passed on my fresh kkt_audit_F2 database; the 94 ERP unit tests pass locally; counts are 31 integration files / 424 it() blocks (docs say 30 files), 13 acceptance files / 55 tests, 24 api unit tests. Gaps confirmed: no coverage configuration in any vitest config or ci.yml; ERP tests are never run by CI; web and portal test scripts are `echo`; browser QA is 41 PNGs plus markdown under docs/qa, not a committed Playwright suite (no playwright dependency in any package.json). One extra finding: running two integration files concurrently races on CREATE DATABASE (I hit `duplicate key value violates unique constraint pg_database_datname_index`), which is why the suite is forced to --no-file-parallelism — the integration/acceptance layers therefore cannot be parallelised without per-worker databases, keeping CI feedback slow. Slightly lower than the first auditor for the un-run ERP suite and unmeasured coverage on a 24k-line API.

**World-best would.** Run every package's tests in CI with v8 coverage gates per package, add per-file schema isolation (template database or per-worker schemas) so integration files run in parallel, commit the Playwright flows as a CI e2e stage against a seeded container, and add contract tests that replay the OpenAPI document against the frontends' typed clients.

Evidence: `apps/api/test/integration/helpers.ts:5-11,90-126 — buildTestApp() creates its own database on demand, migrates, truncates every table, flushes Redis and drives the app via app.inject; vitest.config.ts pins kkt_api_test / Redis db 1; no live server or REACT_APP_BACKEND_URL anywhere; loader.ts:58-63 KKT_MODULES lets a file boot only its module`, `Counts verified: contracts 182 and domain 124 pass (I ran both); conventions (7), idempotency (4) and central (6) integration files pass on a fresh kkt_audit_F database; integration suite = 424 it() across 31 files (docs/STATUS.md:21 says 439/30 — minor drift), acceptance = 55 it() in 13 files with 201 SPEC L… citations`, `apps/api/test/acceptance/harness.ts:4-15,114-136 — each scenario file boots and seeds the demo in-process (or remote via ACCEPTANCE_BASE_URL with POST /admin/reseed), never depends on another file; 08-flexi uses as_of instead of wall-clock; grep finds no sleep/setTimeout/fake timers in test/unit, test/integration or test/acceptance`, `Unit layer is thin at the API edge: apps/api/test/unit has 24 tests (list.test.ts covers only escapeLike/resolveSort); apps/erp has 94 unit tests (api client, math, markdown) but .github/workflows/ci.yml:30-33 never runs pnpm --filter @kkt/erp test; web/portal have no tests (package.json test scripts echo)`, `No coverage tooling or thresholds anywhere (grep 'coverage' across vitest configs and ci.yml: 0); suites run with --no-file-parallelism on one shared database (apps/api/package.json:14-16), acceptance ≈ 6–7 min (docs/ACCEPTANCE.md)`, `Scenario 14 (browser quality bar) is a documented Playwright QA stage (docs/qa/*.md) but not a committed, CI-run e2e suite; adversarial files exist for 8 modules (test/integration/*-adversarial.test.ts)`

### F9. CI/CD, linting, formatting, type-checking — 570/1000 (v1: 165)

**Strengths.** A real GitHub Actions pipeline exercises the whole vertical on every push — typecheck, unit, migrations plus database invariants, integration and the 13 acceptance scenarios against live Postgres/Redis — and the TypeScript configuration is maximally strict across all seven packages.

**Gaps.** There is no linter at all: 'lint' is an alias for tsc, the portal's expo lint cannot run, and Prettier exists only as a config file while a sample of core files is already unformatted. CI skips ERP unit tests, the portal build, formatting, coverage and dependency auditing, and there is no CD stage (image build, push, migrate, deploy) despite four Dockerfiles and a compose file. A single job with no caching or cancellation will be slow (acceptance alone ≈ 7 min) and offers no artefacts.

**Second auditor.** The pipeline in .github/workflows/ci.yml is real and runs typecheck, contracts/domain/db/api unit, integration, three builds and acceptance against live Postgres/Redis, and tsconfig.base.json is maximally strict. But the linting/formatting picture is worse than the first auditor measured: no ESLint config or dependency exists anywhere, every `lint` script is `tsc --noEmit` (apps/api:12, erp:11, web:10, packages/*), the portal's `expo lint` has no config, and `prettier --check` over apps/api/src, packages/contracts/src and packages/domain/src reports 169 unformatted files (not 27). CI omits `pnpm --filter @kkt/erp test` (94 tests), the portal build, format check, coverage, dependency audit, and there is no CD job despite four Dockerfiles and docker-compose. README.md:33 claims 'CI runs all of them' including `pnpm test` for every package, which is not true. The combination of an entirely absent linter, a misleading README claim and a formatting baseline that has already drifted across 169 files justifies a lower score than the first auditor's.

**World-best would.** Add typescript-eslint (strict + import/no-cycle + security rules) and prettier --check as CI gates, run every package's tests with coverage thresholds, add dependency review and secret scanning, split CI into cached parallel jobs, and finish the pipeline with image builds, migration dry-runs and an environment-gated deploy.

Evidence: `.github/workflows/ci.yml:1-37 — one job on push/PR: Postgres 16 + Redis 7 services, pnpm install --frozen-lockfile, pnpm typecheck, contracts/domain tests, db migrate + test, api unit + integration, api/erp/web builds, acceptance scenarios`, `tsconfig.base.json:3-19 — strict, noUncheckedIndexedAccess, noImplicitOverride, verbatimModuleSyntax, isolatedModules; every package has typecheck: tsc --noEmit and docs/STATUS.md reports it green for all 7`, `No ESLint anywhere: every package's lint script is tsc --noEmit (apps/api/package.json:12, apps/erp:11, apps/web:10, packages/*/package.json); apps/portal lint: expo lint has no eslint config or dependency (ls eslint.config.js/.eslintrc.js: none; no eslint in package.json); pnpm lint is not in ci.yml`, `Prettier is configured (.prettierrc) but not enforced: pnpm exec prettier --check on apps/api/src/lib, plugins and packages/contracts/src reports 27 unformatted files; ci.yml has no format check`, `ci.yml omits pnpm --filter @kkt/erp test (94 tests) and the portal build; no coverage upload, no pnpm audit/dependency review, no Dependabot/Renovate (.github contains only workflows/ci.yml), no concurrency cancel, no turbo remote cache, no CD job (Dockerfiles for 4 apps exist but nothing builds/pushes images or deploys)`, `Repo history shows real commits (git log: 60490f7 …) but there is no evidence here of a CI run or branch protection`

### F10. Developer onboarding — 770/1000 (v1: 300)

**Strengths.** A newcomer can clone, run four commands and log into a fully seeded demo builder with documented credentials, because the API self-migrates and self-seeds with safe dev defaults and the docs (README, ARCHITECTURE, DEPLOY, SEED, ACCEPTANCE, STATUS) are current and cross-linked; everything runs on plain Postgres/Redis or docker compose with no proprietary platform.

**Gaps.** The headline step `cp .env.example .env` is inert for the pnpm commands — no code loads an env file — so a developer who edits the root .env to point at another database or disable seeding is silently ignored; the frontend variables in the root example are never read there, and each app README gives a different file name. There is no one-shot bootstrap (`pnpm setup`), no CONTRIBUTING/PR checklist, and the test-selection knobs live in test code rather than docs; STATUS already drifts (30 vs 31 integration files).

**Second auditor.** The happy path is genuinely short and self-contained: README 'Run locally' (4 commands), config.ts dev defaults for DATABASE_URL/REDIS_URL/secrets, RUN_MIGRATIONS_ON_BOOT and SEED_ON_BOOT default true, docs/SEED.md credentials, docker-compose for infra, no proprietary platform. Confirmed the first auditor's main gap: grep for dotenv / --env-file / envFile across apps, packages and Dockerfiles returns nothing, and `dev` is plain `tsx watch src/server.ts`, so `cp .env.example .env` is consumed only by docker compose variable substitution; a developer who edits root .env to change DATABASE_URL, disable seeding or raise log level is silently ignored by `pnpm api`/`pnpm db:migrate`. The root .env.example also carries VITE_/NEXT_PUBLIC_/EXPO_PUBLIC_ variables that only the per-app files read, while the three app READMEs each name a different env file. STATUS.md already drifts (30 vs 31 integration files) and README overstates CI. Minor extra deduction for the README/CI inaccuracy; otherwise the first auditor's assessment holds.

**World-best would.** Load the root .env explicitly (node --env-file or a tiny env loader in server/migrate/vite/next configs), keep one authoritative env table per surface, add `pnpm setup` that installs, migrates, seeds and prints the logins, and ship CONTRIBUTING.md with the module layout, testing switches and a PR checklist that mirrors the CI gates.

Evidence: `README.md 'Run locally' — pnpm install, cp .env.example .env, pnpm db:migrate, pnpm api/erp/web/portal with demo logins; docs/DEPLOY.md:3-14 repeats it with docker compose up postgres redis; docker-compose.yml provides postgres/redis/api/worker/erp/web/portal/caddy; Dockerfiles exist for all four apps`, `apps/api/src/config.ts:57-67 dev-only defaults for JWT_SECRET/FIELD_ENCRYPTION_KEY/HASH_KEY so the API boots with no .env; server.ts migrate()+seed() run on boot (RUN_MIGRATIONS_ON_BOOT, SEED_ON_BOOT); docs/SEED.md lists every demo login, master, project, booking; root scripts db:migrate/db:seed/db:reset/loadtest (package.json:9-21)`, `Root .env.example documents every API variable inline; per-app .env.example and READMEs exist for erp, web, portal (apps/erp/README.md, apps/web/README.md, apps/portal/README.md)`, `Nothing loads a .env file: grep for dotenv / --env-file / envFile across apps, packages, Dockerfiles and package.json scripts finds no hits, so cp .env.example .env is only consumed by docker compose substitution; pnpm api and pnpm db:migrate rely on the dev defaults and ignore the root .env`, `Root .env.example carries VITE_API_URL / NEXT_PUBLIC_* / EXPO_PUBLIC_* that Vite, Next and Expo only read from their own app directories, while the app READMEs say .env.local (erp, web) or apps/portal/.env — three conflicting instructions for the same variables`, `No Emergent or hosted-platform dependency anywhere; node/pnpm pinned (.nvmrc 22, packageManager pnpm@10.28.0); no CONTRIBUTING, PR template or ADR index; KKT_MODULES, ACCEPTANCE_* and TEST_* switches are documented only in docs/ACCEPTANCE.md and test helpers`

## Group G — Features & Depth — **8355 / 10,000** (previous implementation: 6740)

| # | Factor | Previous (v1) | First score | Final score |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Master data & "everything is a selection" principle | 690 | 850 | **845** |
| 2 | Inventory grid & block wizard | 725 | 860 | **820** |
| 3 | Customer 360, KYC workflow, portal access card | 675 | 840 | **800** |
| 4 | Leads pipeline, round robin, website dedupe, follow-ups widget, kanban, convert → booking — routers/leads.py, Leads.jsx | 610 | 830 | **845** |
| 5 | Booking wizard depth | 700 | 850 | **845** |
| 6 | Reports, dashboards, PDFs | 660 | 820 | **830** |
| 7 | Website & CMS & SEO | 640 | 830 | **840** |
| 8 | Brochure lead gate & site-visit scheduler | 680 | 880 | **890** |
| 9 | Customer portal web + mobile | 720 | 860 | **855** |
| 10 | Notifications | 640 | 790 | **785** |

### G1. Master data & "everything is a selection" principle — 845/1000 (v1: 690)

**Strengths.** Every structural master in SPEC §3 exists behind one CRUD factory with company scoping, keyset/offset lists, CSV export, audited create/update/deactivate/restore and a real usage check that blocks deactivation with a structured breakdown; the 'selection' principle is enforced server-side because flat types, templates and plans validate referenced ids and snapshot names at write time. CSV import is a genuine preview/commit flow with duplicate-key detection and atomic commit.

**Gaps.** CSV import works on flat scalar columns only: nested kinds (flat-type rooms/areas, template positions, plan installments) must be supplied as JSON cells with raw UUIDs, so a real bulk load of flat types cannot reference room/area types by code (importer.ts:41-56 coerceCsvValue). The preview marks a row UPDATE but does not show a field-level diff of what will change, and import of a 2 MB file runs synchronously inside the request. Usage checks count direct references only (e.g. a PLC type referenced from a flat's price breakup snapshot is not counted, by design but undocumented). Site-visit settings live on the project rather than as a master, which matches the spec but means they lack the factory's CSV/usage tooling.

**Second auditor.** Verified: 16 kinds registered through one factory (apps/api/src/modules/masters/kinds.ts:79-463, ERP mirrors all 16 in masterConfig.ts), every kind declares a usage() query (kinds.ts:83-463) and deactivate runs usageOf() inside the transaction and throws 409 IN_USE with the breakdown (factory.ts:172-175); importer re-resolves keys inside the commit transaction and refuses commit with ERROR rows unless partial=true (importer.ts:112-124); masters.test.ts re-run 25/25 on kkt_audit_G. The first auditor's gaps hold: nested kinds can only be imported as JSON cells of raw UUIDs (importer.ts:41-56 coerceCsvValue), the preview shows action but no field diff, the whole 2 MB max body (packages/contracts/src/modules/masters.ts:428) is parsed and committed synchronously in the request, and usage counts are direct references only (e.g. charge types count booking_other_charges but not project charge defaults). Slightly lowered because flat-type bulk load by code is the one import a builder actually needs and it is impractical as built.

**World-best would.** Resolve foreign keys by code/name during CSV import with a per-row, per-field diff preview, a downloadable error file and background commit for large files; expose a 'where used' drill-down that lists the actual referencing records and lets the user open them.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/modules/masters/kinds.ts:78-468 — 16 master kinds (room/area/block types, amenities, charge types, document types, PLC types, spec categories+items, service categories, flat types, floor-plan templates, construction stages, payment plans, payment modes, bank accounts) registered through one factory`, `apps/api/src/modules/masters/factory.ts:165-186 — deactivate runs usageOf() first and throws 409 IN_USE with usage_count/used_by; restore reverses with optimistic version; both audited`, `apps/api/src/modules/masters/importer.ts:97-131 — CSV preview classifies rows CREATE/UPDATE/SKIP/ERROR, re-resolves keys inside the commit transaction, refuses commit with ERROR rows unless partial=true (422 IMPORT_HAS_ERRORS)`, `apps/api/src/modules/masters/kinds.ts:184-245 — flat types validate room/area ids exist and are active (400/422), snapshot names/codes, derive carpet/built-up/super from area codes and run validateFlatType (packages/domain/src/flatGeneration.ts:236-253: ≥1 room, ≥1 area, super ≥ built-up ≥ carpet)`, `apps/api/src/modules/masters/kinds.ts:292-330 — exactly one possession stage enforced in prepare(), POST /reorder rewrites sort order; apps/erp/src/features/masters/UsageDialog.tsx:24-80 and CsvImportDialog.tsx:36-120 give the ERP 'Where used' and preview-then-commit UI`, `apps/api/test/integration/masters.test.ts:39-293 — 25 tests (CRUD/version/usage, kind rules, CSV preview+commit) re-run green on kkt_audit_g`

### G2. Inventory grid & block wizard — 820/1000 (v1: 725)

**Strengths.** The grid is a faithful stacked tower with refuge/podium rows, three colour modes with legend counts, hover detail, a rich drawer and real multi-select bulk operations wired to permission-gated endpoints; the wizard covers every step the spec lists with a live stacked preview and price preview from the active rate card, and the generation itself is atomic and acceptance-tested (52 flats, skip 13, refuge 7).

**Gaps.** The spec's 'preview grid' and 'generate' are merged into one review step and the stacked preview lives in the ranges step, so the final confirmation shows counts rather than the exact grid. The grid has no keyboard arrow navigation between cells (role=grid but no roving tabindex) and no virtualisation, so a 60-floor × 12-position tower renders 700+ buttons at once. 'Print availability chart' is browser print CSS over the scroll area rather than a paginated branded PDF, so very wide towers clip in landscape. Bulk price edit only offers rate×area or a flat price; per-floor PLC re-pricing from the rate card requires the separate 'apply rate card' flow.

**Second auditor.** Verified the grid (print CSS + window.print, colour-by, shift/ctrl multi-select, bulk bar, HoldDialog, BulkPriceDialog: InventoryGrid.tsx:83-93,124,171,292-297), the drawer tabs with History timeline (FlatDrawer.tsx:77,132-135), the five wizard steps with expected count (BlockWizard.tsx:51-56,151,316) and the API bulk-status/bulk-price endpoints that skip Booked/Sold and foreign holds (blocks.ts:395-460). What the first auditor missed: a HOLD has a hold_until (default from settings) but nothing ever releases it — there is no expiry job in JOB_SCHEDULES (apps/api/src/jobs/index.ts:29-35), no effective-status check anywhere (grep for hold_until comparisons returns only the 'must be in the future' validator, flats.ts:90-93), so after the window lapses the flat stays HOLD, the public grid shows it 'not available', the booking wizard lets only the holder or a settings.manage user book it (money/services/bookings.ts:88-91) and other reps get 403 (flats.ts:103-105). Also the ERP wizard does not call POST /blocks/preview (the API endpoint is unused; the preview is client math in inventoryMath), bulk-status is not registered idempotent, and the grid renders every cell with no virtualisation or arrow-key navigation. The hold-expiry hole is an inventory-correctness gap, so I lowered the score.

**World-best would.** Render the grid as a virtualised, keyboard-navigable canvas with a server-generated branded availability PDF, let the wizard show the exact generated grid with prices before committing, and offer undo of a bulk status/price change.

Evidence: `apps/erp/src/features/inventory/InventoryGrid.tsx:84-93,164-171,199-273 — print CSS + window.print() chart, colour-by select (STATUS/FLAT_TYPE/PRICE_BAND), floors sorted descending, refuge/podium bands, Tooltip with price/type/facing/customer/hold-until, sticky floor headers`, `apps/erp/src/features/inventory/InventoryGrid.tsx:123-141,177-213 — select mode + shift/ctrl multi-select + click-floor-to-select, bulk bar with status dropdown (Hold dialog with hours/reason) and BulkPriceDialog (rate × super built-up with floor-rise step, re-prices Available only)`, `apps/erp/src/features/inventory/FlatDrawer.tsx:132-261,331-394 — drawer tabs Details (snapshot rooms/areas, specs by category), Price (breakup with mismatch notice), Actions (status/hold, flat-type change only while Available, price), History timeline`, `apps/erp/src/features/inventory/BlockWizard.tsx:51-56,154-178,262-383 — five steps (type → floors & skips with click-to-cycle unit/skip/refuge/podium → ranges with stacked preview → pricing & stages preview → review & generate) with per-step validation and expected flat count`, `apps/api/src/modules/inventory/blocks.ts:175-200,342-460 — POST /blocks/preview, GET /blocks/:id/grid (legend by status/type/price band), bulk-status (flat.status) and bulk-price (flat.write) endpoints; apps/api/test/integration/inventory.test.ts:232,404 cover the grid shape and bulk ops`, `docs/qa/erp.md:22-23 — browser QA exercised wizard at 1440 and 390 px (12 flats generated), colour modes, drawer Hold/Available, select-mode bulk Blocked, print chart`

### G3. Customer 360, KYC workflow, portal access card — 800/1000 (v1: 675)

**Strengths.** The 360 endpoint and page cover every panel the spec lists, KYC is masked by default with an audited reveal and a verifier-recorded status change that only kyc_view holders can make, duplicate detection works on phone and PAN hash, and the portal access card implements enable/reset/disable/revoke with real session-family revocation, one-time password display and a bulk enable that is scope-aware and never persists passwords.

**Gaps.** KYC is a status field, not a workflow: any staff with customer.write can move Pending→Submitted without attaching a PAN/Aadhaar document, there is no required-document checklist, and VERIFIED→SUBMITTED or REJECTED→VERIFIED transitions are unrestricted (customers.ts:216-236). Bulk enable returns every temporary password in one JSON response and the ERP shows them in a toast-driven flow (CustomersPage.tsx:44-162) rather than a printable/exportable credential sheet, and browser QA did not exercise it. The 'share login link' is a WhatsApp deep link composed client-side with the plaintext temp password; nothing records that the credential was handed over. The leads card on the 360 matches by phone heuristically rather than through an explicit customer↔lead link history.

**Second auditor.** Verified the KYC route (customers.ts:216-236: VERIFIED/REJECTED need customer.kyc_view, verifier recorded, audited, but any transition between SUBMITTED/VERIFIED/REJECTED is allowed and no document evidence is required), the 360 query (customers.ts:52-116, bookings/payments scope-filtered, leads matched by customer_id OR phone), portal enable/re-enable with hash computed outside the row lock and family revocation (portalAccess.ts:26-55), and bulk-enable returning plaintext credentials once with an ids-only audit (portalAccess.ts:116-162); crm.test.ts re-run 17/17. The first auditor under-stated the bulk-enable problem: the ERP mutation discards the response's credentials entirely — useMutationWithToast only renders 'Portal enabled for N customers' (CustomersPage.tsx:44-48) and the confirm dialog tells staff to 'share them from each Customer 360 page' (CustomersPage.tsx:159), which is impossible because the API deliberately never stores the temporary password (portalAccess.ts:100-102). Bulk enable therefore produces N logins nobody can use until each is individually reset — the feature is effectively broken end to end, and QA did not exercise it (docs/qa/erp.md 'Not covered'). Lowered accordingly.

**World-best would.** Model KYC as a state machine with document evidence requirements, re-verification on identifier change and a reviewer queue; deliver portal credentials through an audited channel (OTP/magic link) instead of plaintext bulk responses, and keep a per-customer timeline that unifies leads, visits, bookings, payments and tickets.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/modules/crm/customers.ts:120-376 — customers CRUD, POST /duplicate-check (phone + PAN hash), POST /:id/kyc (SUBMITTED/VERIFIED/REJECTED; VERIFIED/REJECTED require customer.kyc_view and record verifier+timestamp, audited), co-applicants CRUD, GET /:id/360, deactivate blocked by active bookings (409 IN_USE) and auto-disables the portal login, POST /:id/erase`, `apps/api/src/modules/crm/portalAccess.ts:26-85 — enable/re-enable (temp password hashed outside the row lock, must_change_password, old hash to history, all refresh families revoked), reset-password, disable, revoke-sessions, each audited; routes deliberately non-idempotent so the one-time password is never stored (lines 100-108)`, `apps/api/src/modules/crm/portalAccess.ts:116-162 — POST /customers/portal/bulk-enable: every customer (primary or co-applicant) with an ACTIVE booking in scope, dry_run counts, per-customer short transactions, credentials returned once, audit row carries ids only`, `apps/erp/src/features/crm/CustomerDetailPage.tsx:41-103 — 360 page: stats, profile, KYC panel, co-applicants, portal access card, bookings, payments, tickets, documents panel, leads history, audit history (gated on audit.view)`, `apps/erp/src/features/crm/customer/PortalAccessCard.tsx:20-156 — last login, enabled on, active sessions, locked-until, must-change flag; enable/reset/revoke/disable with confirm dialogs; temp password shown once with copy, login link and WhatsApp share text`, `apps/api/test/integration/crm.test.ts:276-606 — KYC encryption/masking/audited reveal, duplicate 409, Verhoeff, co-applicants, portal card, bulk enable, 360 (17 tests re-run green)`

### G4. Leads pipeline, round robin, website dedupe, follow-ups widget, kanban, convert → booking — routers/leads.py, Leads.jsx — 845/1000 (v1: 610)

**Strengths.** The pipeline has the right stage rules with history and activities, round robin is a Redis-backed rotation over active Sales users switchable from settings, website/brochure/visit sources all funnel through one dedupe routine serialised by an advisory lock, follow-ups have a dedicated endpoint plus strip and dashboard widget, and the Kanban is a working drag-and-drop with the Lost-reason guard; convert hands off into the booking wizard with the customer and project prefilled.

**Gaps.** Convert does not actually start a booking server-side or create a draft; it only emits a link, so the 'optionally starts a booking' part of the spec is a client affordance. The Kanban's pointer drag is not usable on touch screens (QA fell back to Move-to) and there is no per-column pagination, so a pipeline with thousands of open leads loads every card. Round robin is a plain counter with no per-project rotation list, weighting, or skip-when-on-leave, and ERP-created leads bypass the dedupe (lead.write create uses onExisting conflict only when asked). Lead activities are not surfaced as a unified timeline on the customer record after conversion.

**Second auditor.** Three of the first auditor's gaps are wrong on inspection. (a) POST /leads/:id/convert does create a server-side booking draft when start_booking=true (leads.ts:313-317 inserts a bookingDrafts row at step 2 with lead/customer/project/flat type and returns booking_draft_id) — the ERP simply never asks for it (LeadDrawer.tsx:318-322 hard-codes start_booking:false and links to /bookings/new). (b) ERP-created leads do go through createOrTouchLead with onExisting:'conflict' and get 409 DUPLICATE_LEAD with the existing lead (leads.ts:120, leadService.ts:254-258), so they do not bypass dedupe. (c) The board is not unbounded: counts come from one GROUP BY and cards are top-N per column via row_number() ≤ q.limit (leads.ts:146-151). Round robin is scoped to Sales users whose project scope covers the lead's project (leadService.ts:60-69) with a Postgres sequence fallback when Redis is down. Confirmed real gaps: pointer-only drag (LeadKanban.tsx:62-70; QA used Move-to on the phone), no per-user weighting/leave handling, no response-time SLA, and the ERP ignores the server draft. Raised modestly.

**World-best would.** Offer configurable assignment rules (per project, load-balanced, with availability), touch-friendly Kanban with virtualised columns and WIP counts, lead response-time SLAs with escalation, and a convert flow that creates the customer and a resumable booking draft in one step.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/modules/crm/leadService.ts:238-288 — createOrTouchLead takes a pg_advisory_xact_lock on company+phone+project, reuses the open lead with an activity (website dedupe rule), else creates LD- lead with round-robin assignment when company.settings.round_robin_enabled (nextRoundRobinUser, Redis counter, lines 50-85)`, `apps/api/src/modules/crm/leadService.ts:304-335 — setLeadStage: stage history + STAGE_CHANGED activity + audit, LOST requires reason (422 LOST_REASON_REQUIRED), CONVERTED only via /convert, closed leads cannot move; revertLeadStage restores previous_stage after a visit cancellation`, `apps/api/src/modules/crm/leads.ts:75-348 — list, board (/leads/board), follow-ups due (/leads/follow-ups/due), stage, follow-ups, assign, read/unread, convert (→ customer, idempotent), assignees, unread-count`, `apps/erp/src/features/crm/LeadsPage.tsx:21-166 and LeadKanban.tsx:41-150 — Kanban (pointer-event drag with Move-to menu) or list, LOST prompts for a reason, follow-ups-due strip ('My follow-ups due today'), lead drawer; apps/erp/src/features/dashboard/SalesDashboard.tsx:34-52 follow-ups widget`, `docs/qa/erp.md:25 — convert → customer with the 'Start booking' link to /bookings/new?customer_id=…&project_id=…; apps/api/test/integration/crm.test.ts:63-230 round robin A,B,A, pipeline rules, dedupe, follow-ups, convert (green)`, `apps/api/src/modules/crm/leads.ts:313-317 — start_booking inserts a booking_drafts row (step 2, payload lead_id/customer_id/project_id/flat_type_id)`

### G5. Booking wizard depth — 845/1000 (v1: 700)

**Strengths.** All six steps exist with the right affordances: find-by-number grid picker, inline customer creation and co-applicants, amount/percent split with exact auto-balance, API-driven live schedule preview with default plan first and acceptance capture including signed-copy upload, charge checklist with GST and parking, a printable review, stepper + sticky summary rail + sticky footer, server-side drafts with a resume banner and a local fallback, and the whole thing was walked on a phone.

**Gaps.** In-app navigation away from a dirty wizard is not intercepted (only the browser beforeunload event, BookingWizard.tsx:139-149); autosave limits the damage but a sidebar click still drops the user out mid-step without a prompt. The wizard never places a Hold on the chosen flat, so a 10-minute data-entry window can lose the flat to another rep (the atomic lock then rejects at confirm). Server drafts have no expiry/cleanup and the resume banner only offers the newest draft rather than a list. There is no booking-level duplicate check (same customer already holds an active booking on the same flat type) and the review step cannot be exported as a PDF, only printed.

**Second auditor.** Verified: the only unsaved-changes guard is a beforeunload listener (BookingWizard.tsx:139-149); no useBlocker/router blocker exists anywhere in apps/erp/src, so in-app navigation silently leaves the wizard (autosave at 1.5 s limits the loss). The wizard never places a HOLD — FlatPickerGrid.tsx:16-18 merely allows Available/Hold cells and defers to the confirm-time check (money/services/bookings.ts:88-91), so two reps can fill the same flat and one loses at step 6. Drafts: GET /booking-drafts returns up to 20 per user (bookings.ts:72-78) and PUT upserts (80-94); there is no expiry/cleanup and the resume banner surfaces only the newest. The step set, inline customer create, percent/amount split with exact remainder and API-driven schedule preview were confirmed by code and by the 390 px QA walk (docs/qa/erp.md Bookings row). Score essentially unchanged; the missing in-app guard is a spec line explicitly asked for.

**World-best would.** Reserve the flat with a timed Hold when the wizard starts, block in-app navigation with a save/discard prompt, keep a drafts list with expiry, and generate the review as the allotment/acceptance PDF so the customer can sign at the desk.

Evidence: `apps/erp/src/features/bookings/wizard/BookingWizard.tsx:53-72,86-120,263-335 — six steps (flat, customer, agreement, plan, charges, review), resume banner from server drafts or local copy with discard, ?draft deep link, vertical stepper on desktop / horizontal on phones, sticky footer Back/Next with per-step error count, SummaryRail with autosave status`, `apps/erp/src/features/bookings/wizard/useBookingDraft.ts:56-130 — debounced autosave (1.5 s) to PUT/POST /booking-drafts plus localStorage fallback, flush before leaving, discard deletes the server draft; apps/api/test/integration/money.test.ts:279 covers drafts per user`, `apps/erp/src/features/bookings/bookingMath.ts:20-75 — cash/account split from amounts or percents with the other bucket as exact remainder, rebalance on total change, splitIsExact guard; apps/erp/src/features/bookings/__tests__/bookingMath.test.ts`, `apps/erp/src/features/bookings/wizard/StepCustomer.tsx:57-192 — search by phone/name/code, 'Create customer' opens CustomerFormSheet inline (seeded with the typed phone), co-applicant picker; StepPlan.tsx:17-96 — applicable plans default-first, live SchedulePreviewTable from the API preview, acceptance mode (in person / signed copy upload / portal) and date`, `apps/erp/src/features/bookings/wizard/BookingWizard.tsx:139-149 — unsaved-changes guard is beforeunload only (no router blocker: grep useBlocker returns nothing in apps/erp/src)`, `docs/qa/erp.md:28 — wizard completed as admin at 1440 and as Sales on the 390 px phone emulation (grid pick, phone search, 60/40 via percentages, Standard CLP default with 11-row preview, charge + parking, review, ACTIVE booking with catch-up demands)`

### G6. Reports, dashboards, PDFs — 830/1000 (v1: 660)

**Strengths.** Every report in SPEC §14 exists with strict parameters, totals, CSV and a branded A4 PDF generated by one letterhead engine with Indian formatting, words and page numbers; role dashboards are real server-built payloads with project scoping and caching; presets and the scoped command palette work; and acceptance tests prove the money reports reconcile with the ledger.

**Gaps.** The command palette's 'recent items' is a stub — the API hard-codes recent: [] (search/index.ts:76-80) and the ERP shell keeps no local recents, so half of the spec's palette requirement is missing. Report PDFs are built synchronously inside the request (pdfkit stream) and capped at 2,000 rows, contradicting the architecture note that heavy exports run in workers; there is no async export with download notification. The ERP gates the Activity report link on report.view while the API requires audit.view (deferred TODO-integration item 10), so some roles see a link that 403s. Presets are private per user with no sharing, and there is no scheduled/e-mailed report delivery.

**Second auditor.** Verified the 15-entry catalogue with formats/params/required params (reports.ts:28-44), synchronous PDF streaming via exportTable (reports.ts:57-66) capped at PDF_MAX_ROWS=2000 with a visible note (pdf.ts:523-545), per-user presets guarded by report.view (presets.ts), dashboards cached 15 s (dashboards.ts:21,56), and the search endpoint hard-coding recent: [] (search/index.ts:79-80). The first auditor missed that the ERP palette does keep recents client-side in localStorage (command-palette.tsx:36-41,81,117: kkt.erp.recent, max 8), so the spec's 'recent items' is met at the UI level even though the API field is a stub — raised a little. Confirmed open: /reports/activity requires audit.view (reports.ts:337) while the ERP report definition carries no permission (reports.tsx:691-700; TODO-integration item 10 deferred), so Project Admin/Sales see a link that 403s; the ERP exposes 13 report definitions versus 15 API keys (payment schedule/allotment live under the customer-statement page); no async export, no shared presets, no scheduled delivery.

**World-best would.** Run large CSV/PDF exports as background jobs with progress and a download notification, support shared/team presets and scheduled e-mail delivery, and give the palette true recents, keyboard-first actions ('post payment for BK-…') and fuzzy matching across all entities.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/modules/reports/routes/reports.ts:28-44 — catalogue of 15 reports (collections, outstanding & ageing, advances, demand vs collection, upcoming dues, inventory, customer statement, payment schedule, allotment letter, cheques, cancellations, sales funnel, roznamcha, bank-wise receipts, activity) each declaring csv/pdf formats, params and required params; exportTable streams CSV or a branded PDF (lines 57-66)`, `apps/api/src/modules/reports/services/pdf.ts:2-4,120-142,407-412,454-467 — BrandedPdf: letterhead with logo/GSTIN/RERA, navy title bar, Indian number formatting, amount-in-words block, CANCELLED watermark, 'Page x of y' + generated-at IST footer; PDF_MAX_ROWS 2000 with visible truncation note (523-526)`, `apps/api/src/modules/reports/routes/presets.ts:28-67 — per-user saved presets (list/create/delete, 409 on duplicate name, audited); apps/api/src/modules/reports/services/dashboards.ts:28-65 — OWNER/ACCOUNTS/SALES/PROJECT_ADMIN/EMPLOYEE dashboards chosen by role or permission heuristic, cached 15 s per user+scope`, `apps/api/src/modules/search/index.ts:29-80 — Ctrl/⌘K search across projects/blocks/flats/customers/bookings with project scope, but recent: [] is always returned (no recent-items implementation)`, `apps/erp/src/features/reports/ReportCharts.tsx:29-156 — Recharts views (collections, ageing, demand vs collection, velocity, funnel, source conversion); docs/qa/erp.md:34 — 12 report keys plus customer statement page, CSV + PDF downloads verified (%PDF header)`, `apps/api/test/integration/reports.test.ts:319-628 — every report answers JSON/CSV/PDF; receipt words/watermark, demand letter buckets + bank account, allotment/statement PDFs, presets, dashboards per role; acceptance 13 reconciles reports with the ledger`

### G7. Website & CMS & SEO — 840/1000 (v1: 640)

**Strengths.** All SPEC §15 pages, per-project toggles, the hard publish guard, published-content-only sitemap/robots, JSON-LD on project/blog/list pages, the site-live coming-soon switch, click-to-load embeds and the sticky mobile sheet are implemented and browser-verified; the public API is allow-listed, cached and collapses non-available statuses, and the CMS inside the ERP covers settings, banners, sections, testimonials, gallery, posts, pages, people, notices and the enquiries inbox.

**Gaps.** Lists and site settings sit behind two 60 s caches (API Redis + Next Data Cache) with no revalidateTag from the CMS, so a publish/unpublish or site_live flip can show the old list once up to a minute later (docs/qa/web.md:67). Structured data stops at Project/Article/FAQ/Breadcrumb — there is no Organization/LocalBusiness/WebSite schema on the home page and no OG image generation. Each project/post render issues the detail request twice (metadata + body) because detail fetches are no-store. Phone numbers render unformatted and seed media are placeholders, which the QA notes but does not fix. There is no preview-before-publish for CMS pages/posts and no scheduled publishing for posts (banners have schedules).

**Second auditor.** All spec pages exist under apps/web/app (home, projects, projects/[slug], about, gallery, blog, blog/[slug], contact with VisitBooking, visit/[token], privacy, disclaimer, not-found, sitemap.ts, robots.ts). The first auditor's claim that there is no Organization/WebSite schema is wrong: the root layout injects organizationJsonLd + websiteJsonLd on every page (apps/web/app/layout.tsx:101), and every list/detail page adds Breadcrumb JSON-LD (projects/page.tsx:89, blog/page.tsx:84, page.tsx:117 etc.). Confirmed gaps: no revalidateTag/revalidatePath call anywhere in apps/web or the CMS routes (grep returns only the 60 s revalidate option in lib/api.ts:153-157), so list pages and site_live can lag up to a minute (docs/qa/web.md notes this); no opengraph-image/ImageResponse generation; availability grid collapses non-Available statuses and hides prices unless show_prices (publicContent.ts:259-263); brochure link verification checks signature, hash row, expiry, publish state and PDF mime (publicForms.ts:192-204). Raised for the structured-data correction.

**World-best would.** Trigger tag-based revalidation from every CMS write so public pages update instantly, add Organization/LocalBusiness/WebSite schema and generated OG images, support draft preview links and scheduled publishing for every content type, and serve images through a CDN image loader with AVIF/WebP negotiation.

Evidence: `apps/web/app — (site)/page, projects, projects/[slug], about, gallery, blog, blog/[slug], contact, visit/[token], (legal)/privacy + disclaimer, not-found, sitemap.ts, robots.ts; components/layout/StickyMobileBar.tsx:12-60 Call · WhatsApp · Enquire sheet; ui/ClickToLoadEmbed.tsx click-to-load video/tour/map`, `apps/api/src/modules/web/projectWebsite.ts:70-80,134-157 — publishBlockers (hero image, description, location) → 422 PUBLISH_REQUIREMENTS; packages/contracts/src/modules/cms.ts:348-351 show_prices/show_availability/starting_price_override; cms.ts:69-70 site_live + coming_soon_message`, `apps/web/app/sitemap.ts:9-45 and robots.ts:8-19 — built only from published projects/posts, legal-only sitemap and Disallow: / while site_live=false; apps/web/app/(site)/projects/[slug]/page.tsx:410-414 — Project, Breadcrumb and FAQ JSON-LD; blog/[slug]/page.tsx:84 Article JSON-LD`, `apps/api/src/modules/web/publicContent.ts:261-265,338 — availability grid collapses HOLD/BOOKED/BLOCKED/SOLD to not-available and withholds prices unless show_prices; 462-608 allow-listed public routes with 60 s cache; apps/api/src/modules/web/cms.ts:91-478 site settings (brand, contacts, offices, social, trust stats, disclaimer, SEO defaults, analytics, round-robin mirror, site_live) and CMS collections + media library`, `docs/qa/web.md:13-45 — 39 page audits at 1440/390 with settle < 1 s, unpublish → 404 within ~50 ms after fixing Next's Data Cache (defect 2), coming-soon and robots/sitemap toggles verified; apps/api/test/acceptance/11-website-publish-enquiries.test.ts`, `apps/web/app/layout.tsx:101 — <JsonLd data={[organizationJsonLd(site, config.apiUrl), websiteJsonLd(site)]} />`

### G8. Brochure lead gate & site-visit scheduler — 890/1000 (v1: 680)

**Strengths.** This is the most complete area in the group: the brochure gate is cryptographically signed, counted once, dedupes leads, remembers the device and feeds a 30-day sparkline; availability is a pure, unit-tested IST algorithm; the last seat is protected by an advisory lock with a concurrent test; the .ics carries a real VTIMEZONE and reminder; the manage link cancels, frees the seat and reverts the lead stage; and the ERP has day/week calendar, capacity-checked reschedule, outcomes and a daily nudge job.

**Gaps.** Manage tokens never expire (visitService.ts:185-201 looks up by permanent sha256 hash), so a shared link can cancel a visit indefinitely; the visitor cannot reschedule from the manage page, only cancel. Availability responses are cached 60 s so the public date strip may still show a seat that just filled until the server recheck rejects it. The ERP calendar keeps separate project filters for calendar and list views (docs/qa/erp.md:61) and offers no drag-to-reschedule. Confirmation is in-app/ics only — no e-mail or WhatsApp delivery of the .ics, which the spec scopes out but which a builder will expect.

**Second auditor.** Re-ran visits.test.ts on kkt_audit_G: 12/12 green including the concurrent last-seat test. The first auditor's 'availability cached 60 s may show a seat that just filled' is wrong: the API busts the public availability cache after every public booking and cancel (publicVisits.ts:49-52,115,163) and after every ERP create/reschedule/cancel (visitRoutes.ts:45-47,171,218,234), and the website fetches availability with revalidate:0 (apps/web/lib/api.ts:222-231), so the strip is fresh on the next request. Confirmed real gaps: manage tokens are looked up by a permanent sha256 hash with no expiry or rotation (visitService.ts:186-189) and the only public token actions are view/.ics/cancel (publicVisits.ts:127-150) — no visitor self-reschedule; list/calendar project filters are separate (docs/qa/erp.md open notes); no e-mail/WhatsApp delivery of the .ics. Raised slightly for the cache correction.

**World-best would.** Expire or rotate manage tokens, allow visitor self-reschedule, bust the availability cache per slot on write, add drag-to-reschedule on the staff calendar with assignee lanes, and send calendar invites and reminders through e-mail/WhatsApp with delivery tracking.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/modules/web/publicForms.ts:44-69,128-201 — HMAC-signed 15-minute brochure token stored by sha256 hash, visitor token (180 d) for returning devices, honeypot returns a believable never-issued link, lead reuse via createOrTouchLead (source BROCHURE), GET /public/brochure/:token verifies signature+expiry+row, streams the PDF as attachment and counts once per link`, `apps/api/src/modules/web/projectWebsite.ts:82-90,223-243 — brochure_download_count plus zero-filled 30-day sparkline and /projects/:id/brochure-stats`, `packages/domain/src/visits.ts:66-105 — pure availability(): visit days, blackouts, now + min lead hours in IST, max days ahead, seats − non-cancelled count, only slots with available > 0; 156-186 VTIMEZONE Asia/Kolkata, DTSTART;TZID=Asia/Kolkata, TRIGGER:-PT1H, RFC 5545 folding`, `apps/api/src/modules/web/visitService.ts:119-135,290-330 — per-slot advisory lock + recount → 409 SLOT_FULL, then lead create/touch (SITE_VISIT), visit SV- number, manage token hash; 246-250 revertLeadAfterCancel; 415-435 daily VISIT_OUTCOME_NUDGE once per visit per day`, `apps/api/src/modules/web/visitRoutes.ts:58-273 — ERP list/csv, calendar, availability, today, create, PATCH notes/assignee, reschedule (capacity-checked), cancel with reason, complete/no-show outcome; apps/erp/src/features/crm/visits/VisitCalendar.tsx:39-66 day/week views with 'moved' tag; SlotPicker hides full slots`, `apps/api/test/integration/visits.test.ts:74-373 — 12 tests incl. concurrent last-seat (exactly one 201 / one 409), .ics IST + alarm, manage cancel reverts lead stage, reschedule capacity (re-run green on kkt_audit_g); acceptance 12; docs/qa/web.md:43-44 phone booking flow + manage page`

### G9. Customer portal web + mobile — 855/1000 (v1: 720)

**Strengths.** Every screen in SPEC §16 exists in one Expo codebase with a desktop sidebar and phone tab bar, the API enforces ownership through a single member condition that returns 404 for anything else including PDFs and file links, KYC stays masked, construction shows only the buyer's tower and buyer-visible photos, PDFs stream through customer-bound signed links with a native share sheet, and the cached-first behaviour is implemented with a persisted query cache; browser QA and 90 API tests back it.

**Gaps.** Native iOS/Android builds were never produced or run (docs/STATUS.md:33), so camera capture, SecureStore, share sheet and deep links are verified only by code reading. There are no push notifications (bell polls every 60 s, queries.ts:130) and no biometric/PIN re-entry, both expected of a premium consumer app. The offline cache is read-only: mutations are offlineFirst but there is no queued retry UI if a request is sent without connectivity. 'My Flat' has no floor-plan image in the demo and the plan-acceptance-on-portal flow (acceptance mode PORTAL) has no portal screen to actually accept the plan.

**Second auditor.** Verified every screen route under apps/portal/src/app/(app) (home, flat, payments, construction, more/{documents, notices, notices/[id], notifications, profile, password, sessions, bank-details, requests, requests/new, requests/[id]}), the single memberCondition (primary OR co-applicant) feeding loadOwnedBooking → 404 (portal/common.ts:37-78), customer-bound 15-minute signed file links with Redis-indexed temp PDFs and best-effort sweep (portal/files.ts:126-150), offlineFirst persisted TanStack cache with refetchOnMount 'always' (queries.ts:19-45), notification tap → hrefForNotification (NotificationsScreen.tsx:52) and a 60 s unread poll (queries.ts:130). Confirmed gaps: no expo-notifications or LocalAuthentication dependency (no push, no biometric), native builds never run (docs/STATUS.md:33), no portal step to accept a plan when acceptance mode is PORTAL (portal/bookings.ts only displays accepted_on at :159 and the acceptance document at :392-397), floor-plan image absent in seed (docs/qa/portal.md). Score unchanged within noise.

**World-best would.** Ship store builds with push notifications, biometric unlock and deep links, add an in-portal plan/agreement acceptance step with e-sign and audit trail, and make the cache mutation-aware with queued retries and conflict messaging.

Evidence: `apps/portal/src/app/(app) — index (home), flat, payments, construction, more/{documents, notices, notices/[id], notifications, profile, password, sessions, bank-details, requests, requests/new, requests/[id]}, login, change-password, +not-found; screens/*.tsx implement each`, `apps/api/src/modules/portal/common.ts:37-78 — memberCondition (primary or co-applicant) and loadOwnedBooking → 404 for anything else; bookings.ts:438-522 home/flat/ledger/receipts(+pdf)/demands(+pdf)/schedule/statement.pdf/allotment-letter.pdf/construction/documents(+download)/bank-details; files.ts:126-150 customer-bound signed file links`, `apps/portal/src/api/queries.ts:1-40 — TanStack Query persisted to AsyncStorage (7 d), networkMode offlineFirst, refetchOnMount 'always' so cached screens render instantly and refresh; apps/portal/src/app/(app)/_layout.tsx:1-18 — Sidebar at ≥ 1024 px, TabBar on phones`, `apps/portal/src/api/files.tsx:49-51 — native share sheet via expo-sharing for PDFs; files.web.tsx in-app viewer/download; lib/storage.ts:5-25 refresh token in SecureStore; package.json expo-image-picker for camera/library attachments`, `apps/api/test/integration/portal.test.ts:249-522 and portal-adversarial — ownership 404 on every route incl. PDFs, co-applicant access, masked KYC, own-tower/visible-photos construction, shared docs only; acceptance 10`, `docs/qa/portal.md:5-70 — 40 screens at 1440/390, 0 console errors, tab bar never covers content, forged booking id → 404 everywhere, in-app PDF viewer + download, internal notes never rendered, reopen/rate flows; 11 defects found and fixed`

### G10. Notifications — 785/1000 (v1: 640)

**Strengths.** Both bells exist with the full type list, the retraction rule is implemented centrally and invoked by every reversing flow (undo, void, notice delete, visit cancel), overdue/SLA/nudge jobs run once per day or hour via BullMQ, service tickets implement SLA from settings, internal notes, the 7-day reopen window and rating-closes, and documents enforce type applicability, KYC sensitivity/share rules, signature validation, variants and short-lived signed links with strong adversarial tests.

**Gaps.** The spec's startup sweep that removes notifications pointing at deleted entities is not implemented: NOTIFICATION_SWEEP exists only as an enum value with no handler or schedule, and the startup reconciliation job is registered but never run at boot, so stale bells can survive deletions (e.g. soft-deleted documents or erased customers) and the ERP shows 'Withdrawn' badges only for explicitly withdrawn rows. Other-charge demand notifications are keyed by booking (TODO item 6), so a construction undo leaves those bells in place. Notifications have no per-user preferences, digesting or batching (the seed gives Accounts 63 unread bells, docs/qa/erp.md:66), and staff bells for stage completion go to Super Admin + scoped Project Admins only, not Accounts. Documents lack a virus scan and ERP thumbnails are served through 15-minute private links rather than long-lived cached URLs; there is no document versioning or expiry on shared documents.

**Second auditor.** Re-ran notifications.test.ts (2/2) and service.test.ts (19/19) on kkt_audit_G. Verified withdraw() deletes unread and flags read rows (notifications/service.ts:49-53), lists exclude withdrawn by default (notifications/index.ts:42), and it is invoked from demand void (dues.ts:275,289), visit cancel/outcome (visitService.ts:369,409) and notice unpublish/delete (notices.ts:253,271). Confirmed the first auditor's central finding: NOTIFICATION_SWEEP exists only in the JobName enum (packages/contracts/src/enums.ts:790,804) with no registerJob and no schedule, and STARTUP_RECONCILIATION is registered (admin/tools.ts:168) but never enqueued at boot — server.ts/worker.ts contain no reference, worker.ts:41 only runs queued jobs. Also nothing withdraws notifications on document soft-delete or customer erasure (customers.ts erase path touches leads/co-applicants only). Service: SLA breach job flags once with an idempotent UPDATE and notifies assignee + admin roles (service/jobs.ts:19-45), 7-day reopen (ticketService.ts:180), rating only on terminal tickets (184-186), internal notes filtered (227). Documents: type applies-to + KYC forces sensitive/unshareable on create and PATCH (documents/service.ts:81-88, routes.ts:150-151), sharp thumb/medium generated inline on upload with the original kept on rendition failure (service.ts:128-139), sensitive rows hidden without kyc_view (routes.ts:112). Unchanged within noise; other-charge bells keyed by booking (TODO item 6) remains.

**World-best would.** Register and schedule the orphan sweep (and run reconciliation at boot behind a leader lock), key every notification by the precise entity so retraction is exact, add preference-driven grouping/digests and read-sync across devices, and add malware scanning, versioning and share expiry to documents.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/service.ts:24-52 — notify/notifyRole and withdraw(): unread deleted, read flagged withdrawn; index.ts:43,70 counts exclude withdrawn; used by construction undo (construction/service.ts:488), demand void (money/services/dues.ts:275,289), notice delete and visit cancel; packages/contracts/src/enums.ts:641-660 notification types cover every staff and buyer bell in SPEC §13`, `packages/contracts/src/enums.ts:785-794 lists NOTIFICATION_SWEEP and STARTUP_RECONCILIATION, but apps/api/src/jobs/index.ts:29-33 schedules only DAILY_DUES, NIGHTLY_INTEGRITY, OVERDUE_NOTIFICATIONS, VISIT_OUTCOME_NUDGE, SLA_BREACH_CHECK; no registerJob('NOTIFICATION_SWEEP') exists anywhere in apps/api/src and STARTUP_RECONCILIATION (admin/tools.ts:168) is never enqueued at boot (server.ts/worker.ts)`, `apps/api/src/modules/service/ticketService.ts:38-46,177-191,270-273,545-571,633-660 — status machine, SLA hours per priority from settings, internal notes stripped from customer views, 7-day reopen window (409 REOPEN_WINDOW_CLOSED), rating 1–5 once closes a RESOLVED ticket; service/jobs.ts:17-47 hourly SLA breach flag + notify once`, `apps/api/src/modules/documents/service.ts:79-88,110-160,238-262 — document-type applies-to rule, KYC type forces is_sensitive and refuses share (422 KYC_NOT_SHAREABLE), 15 MB + magic bytes, webp thumb 480 / medium 1080, uuid keys, 54-char signed links; routes.ts:95-116 sensitive docs hidden without kyc_view, 163-179 soft delete; web/shared.ts:18 immutable cache for public media`, `docs/TODO-integration.md:13-14 — other-charge demand notifications are keyed by booking, so construction undo cannot withdraw them selectively (deferred)`, `apps/api/test/integration/notifications.test.ts (2 tests re-run green), service.test.ts:122-622 (SLA, internal notes, reopen, rating, breach job, notices), documents.test.ts:95-660 (variants, KYC rules, sensitivity, signed links, soft delete, adversarial uploads)`

## Group H — UX, Quality & Operations — **7090 / 10,000** (previous implementation: 5510)

| # | Factor | Previous (v1) | First score | Final score |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Visual design system & theme fidelity | 670 | 770 | **760** |
| 2 | Responsiveness at 390 px & accessibility | 520 | 690 | **670** |
| 3 | Form UX: validation messages, unsaved-changes guard, autosave, keyboard, dropdown-first data entry | 550 | 720 | **710** |
| 4 | Empty/loading/error states, console errors, resilience | 530 | 780 | **760** |
| 5 | Indian localisation: INR grouping and short forms, amount in words, IST dates, FY numbering | 730 | 770 | **750** |
| 6 | Observability: structured logs, request ids, metrics, health/readiness, tracing, alerting hooks | 490 | 620 | **600** |
| 7 | Deployment architecture | 380 | 600 | **580** |
| 8 | Seed/demo data coherence | 700 | 860 | **850** |
| 9 | Documentation quality & honesty of claims | 510 | 790 | **760** |
| 10 | Overall production readiness for a real builder today | 430 | 680 | **650** |

### H1. Visual design system & theme fidelity — 760/1000 (v1: 670)

**Strengths.** Each surface has a complete, well-structured token system that matches its brief: a dense 36 px-control / 40 px-row ERP with tabular money, an editorial serif site with fluid display type and luxe motion, and a premium ivory/navy portal with tone chips. Brand navy #0B1F3A and gold #C9A227 are the anchors everywhere and the browser QA screenshots confirm the look is applied on every page.

**Gaps.** The three apps hand-maintain three divergent palettes (navy and ink scales disagree by surface) with no shared `@kkt/tokens` package, so brand changes must be made three times and will drift. The ERP loads its fonts from Google Fonts at runtime (no self-hosting, a privacy and intranet concern for a staff app) and the native portal ships system fonts rather than the brand faces. The website's gold-600 eyebrow on ivory (3.19:1) and the portal's ink-subtle captions (3.03:1) are part of the token set itself, so the design system bakes in AA failures. The stylesheet references a design_guidelines.json that is not in the repository.

**Second auditor.** Re-read all three token sets. The first auditor's facts hold: apps/erp/src/styles/index.css:6-70 defines a full navy/gold/status/semantic scale, apps/web/app/globals.css:6-60 a separate scale with navy-900=#0b1f3a while the ERP puts #0b1f3a at navy-950 and #10294d at navy-900, and apps/portal/src/ui/theme.ts:7-37 a third hand-written palette (ink #14213A vs #0f172a vs #0b1f3a). index.css:4 cites a 'legacy design_guidelines.json' that does not exist. The ERP loads Inter/Manrope/JetBrains Mono from Google Fonts at runtime (apps/erp/index.html:11-14) whereas the website self-hosts via next/font (apps/web/app/layout.tsx:2,19,27), a mitigation the first auditor did not credit; the portal explicitly ships Georgia/System on native (theme.ts:40-48).
