Each surface is internally coherent and the QA screenshots confirm application, but the system is three systems, so I shave 10 points for the drift plus the AA-failing pairs that are part of the tokens themselves (see next factor).

**World-best would.** Publish one tokens package (JSON → CSS variables → RN theme) consumed by all three apps with contrast-verified pairs, self-host brand fonts (woff2 subsets, expo-font on native), and document the system in a living style guide with every component state.

Evidence: `apps/erp/src/styles/index.css:5-120 — full Tailwind 4 @theme: navy 50–950 scale (950 = brand #0b1f3a), gold family, ivory/surface tokens, status-chip triplets, 2xs–3xl type scale, radii, --spacing-sidebar 260 / --spacing-topbar 64 / --spacing-control 36 / --spacing-row 40 (dense ERP), shadows, motion keyframes`, `apps/erp/src/styles/index.css:248-252 money utility (tabular-nums) and :198-203 gold :focus-visible ring; apps/erp/index.html:12-16 loads Inter/Manrope/JetBrains Mono from Google Fonts CDN`, `apps/web/app/globals.css:7-100 — separate token set for the site: Cormorant Garamond display + Manrope, fluid clamp() display sizes, eyebrow letter-spacing 0.22em, ease-luxe, ken-burns/fade-up animations (cinematic brief); apps/web/components/ui/Button.tsx:8-23 gold pill buttons min-h-12`, `apps/portal/src/ui/theme.ts:7-37 colors, :40-48 fonts (comment admits native uses Georgia/serif + System until expo-font is wired), :66-82 toneStyles; apps/portal/src/ui/Text.tsx:6-19 type ramp`, `Token drift across surfaces: ERP navy-900 #10294d vs web navy-900 #0b1f3a; ERP navy-950 #0b1f3a vs web navy-950 #06142a; ink #0f172a (ERP) vs #0b1f3a (web) vs #14213A (portal); no shared tokens package; apps/erp/src/styles/index.css:4 cites a 'legacy design_guidelines.json' that does not exist in this repo`, `docs/qa/erp.md:10, docs/qa/web.md:12, docs/qa/portal.md:5 — 40 + 39 + 40 palette-quantised screenshots at 1440/390 confirm the theme renders consistently`

### H2. Responsiveness at 390 px & accessibility — 670/1000 (v1: 520)

**Strengths.** Layout at 390 px is genuinely verified across 622 page audits with automated overflow and tap-target assertions, and the fixes they produced are in the code (h-11 controls, min-h-12 site buttons, tab bar inset). Skip links, landmarks, focus rings, aria-sort, labelled icon buttons and Radix focus traps give the ERP and site a solid keyboard/screen-reader baseline.

**Gaps.** SPEC §18.14 demands WCAG-AA contrast, but it was never measured and the token set fails it in common places: portal captions (3.03:1), web eyebrows and secondary text (3.19–3.96:1), ERP danger buttons (3.76:1), portal gold chips (2.94:1). The ERP targets 44 px, not the 48 px the spec names (met only on the website). The portal's accessibility is thin: inputs are not programmatically labelled, errors are not announced, and only 11 accessibilityLabel calls exist across 17 screens. No automated accessibility tooling (axe, Lighthouse, pa11y) runs in CI, so regressions would be invisible.

**Second auditor.** I recomputed every contrast pair the first auditor listed with the WCAG formula and they are exact: portal inkSubtle #8B95A5 on white 3.03:1, portal gold700 on gold100 (the `gold` tone chip, theme.ts:96) 2.94:1, portal warning on warningBg 3.67:1, web ink-soft on ivory 3.96:1, web gold-600 on ivory 3.19:1, ERP white on #ef4444 danger button 3.76:1; additionally ERP ink-subtle (#64748b) on the page surface (#f5f7fb) is 4.44:1, just under AA. The portal has zero live regions and zero role=alert (grep), Field.tsx:18-46 renders a visual label with no accessibilityLabel on the TextInput, and accessibilityLabel appears in only 11 files. The 390 px layout work is real (docs/qa/erp.md:10 543 audits with scrollWidth and ≥44 px assertions; button.tsx:21-24 h-11 on phones) and skip links exist (AppShell.tsx:95, layout.tsx:87), but the spec's AA bar was never measured and the design tokens themselves fail it in common places, so I go slightly lower than the first auditor.

**World-best would.** Run axe-core/Lighthouse in the Playwright QA and fail CI under AA, fix the failing pairs at the token level, label every RN input and announce form errors with live regions, and test the three apps with VoiceOver/TalkBack and a keyboard-only pass.

Evidence: `docs/qa/erp.md:10 — 543 page audits (156 at 390 px): scrollWidth ≤ innerWidth, 0 console errors, every primary target ≥ 44 px after fixes; docs/qa/web.md:12 — 39 pages, no target < 44 px; docs/qa/portal.md:5 — 40 screens, tab bar at 776–844 of 844 px never covers content`, `apps/erp/src/components/layout/AppShell.tsx:94-95 skip link, :119 <main id="main" tabIndex={-1}>; apps/erp/src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx:57 <nav aria-label="Primary"> with NavLink; apps/erp/src/components/ui/data-table.tsx:293,377 keyboard row handler and aria-sort; apps/erp/src/components/ui/button.tsx:21-24 h-11 (44 px) below sm, h-9 desktop; IconButton requires label (button.tsx:86-104)`, `apps/web/app/layout.tsx:87-92 skip link; apps/web/components/ui/Button.tsx:19-23 min-h-12 (48 px, meets SPEC §15); apps/web/components/layout/SiteNav.tsx:47,94-110 aria-label/aria-expanded/aria-controls, role=dialog menu; apps/web/components/ui/Field.tsx:21-36 label/htmlFor, role=alert error, aria-invalid`, `apps/portal/src/ui/shell/TabBar.tsx:17,25-26 accessibilityRole tablist/tab + selected state; apps/portal/src/ui/Button.tsx:42-44 role/state; but only 11 accessibilityLabel uses in all of apps/portal/src and zero live regions; apps/portal/src/ui/Field.tsx:18-60 TextInput receives no accessibilityLabel for its visible label`, `Computed contrast of shipped token pairs: portal colors.inkSubtle #8B95A5 on white 3.03:1 (53 uses, 12 px captions, Text.tsx:15); portal gold700 on gold100 2.94:1 (gold chips); portal warning on warningBg 3.67:1; web text-ink-soft on ivory 3.96:1 (34 uses incl. filter labels, ProjectFilters.tsx:18-37); web text-gold-600 eyebrow 3.19:1 (32 uses, Section.tsx:40-48); ERP white on #ef4444 danger buttons 3.76:1 (12 uses) — all below WCAG AA 4.5:1`, `docs/qa/erp.md:67 and docs/qa/web.md:69 — 'WCAG contrast measurement (visual check only)'; no axe/pa11y run anywhere in CI`

### H3. Form UX: validation messages, unsaved-changes guard, autosave, keyboard, dropdown-first data entry — 710/1000 (v1: 550)

**Strengths.** Validation is shared with the API through the contracts Zod schemas, errors are accessible and specific, server-side field errors flow back into the form, and the booking wizard implements the spec's autosave/resume with a server draft plus a local fallback. Data entry is selection-first with searchable comboboxes and a money input that understands Indian shorthand, and optimistic-concurrency conflicts get a real dialog naming who changed the record.

**Gaps.** The spec's 'unsaved-changes guard' exists only as a browser beforeunload on two wizards; in-app route changes (sidebar click, Cmd+K jump, browser back) lose edits with no prompt because react-router's blocker is never used, and Radix sheets close on outside click regardless of dirty state. Autosave is limited to the booking wizard; the block wizard, CMS editors and the portal's request composer keep nothing if the tab dies. There is no field-level inline validation timing policy (onBlur vs submit) documented, and keyboard shortcuts beyond Cmd+K and table rows are absent.

**Second auditor.** Confirmed both directions. useBookingDraft.ts:56-75 really does debounce (1.5 s) a contract-shaped payload to /booking-drafts with a localStorage fallback and exposes flush/discard; version-conflict-dialog.tsx:11-19 names changedBy/changedAt with a Reload action; SettingsPage.tsx:213 has a sticky Discard/Save bar with an 'Unsaved changes' label but no navigation guard. The grep for useBlocker|usePrompt|onInteractOutside|onEscapeKeyDown across apps/erp/src returns nothing; only BlockWizard.tsx:140 and BookingWizard.tsx:147 register beforeunload, so in-app navigation from any sheet/form silently discards edits. Validation via shared Zod contracts and accessible error wiring is as described. The first auditor's score is fair; I trim 10 for the absence of any route-level guard in a 52-route SPA where sidebar/⌘K navigation is the normal way to leave a form.

**World-best would.** Provide one `useUnsavedChanges(form)` hook that blocks router navigation, outside-click sheet dismissal and tab close with a Save/Discard dialog; autosave every long form to the server draft API; add onBlur validation and shortcut hints on primary actions.

Evidence: `apps/erp/src/components/ui/form.tsx:44-80 FieldWrapper wires id/aria-invalid/aria-describedby/aria-required and renders errors with role=alert; FormField (:96-120) binds react-hook-form Controller; apps/erp/src/features/shared/contractResolver.ts resolves @kkt/contracts Zod schemas client-side`, `apps/erp/src/features/bookings/wizard/useBookingDraft.ts:56-60 debounced 1.5 s autosave to PUT/POST /booking-drafts with a localStorage fallback; BookingWizard.tsx:88-110 'Resume booking?' candidates (:267), :147 beforeunload guard; BlockWizard.tsx:140 beforeunload`, `Dropdown-first: cmdk Combobox used in 24 feature files; wizard steps use Combobox/Select/RadioGroup (StepCustomer 3, StepPlan 3, StepFlat 2, BlockWizard 5); apps/erp/src/components/ui/number-input.tsx:115-156 MoneyInput types rupees → integer paise with live Indian grouping; apps/erp/src/lib/format.ts:108-140 accepts '45L', '1.2 Cr', '50k'`, `apps/erp/src/app.tsx:8 mounts VersionConflictHost; version-conflict-dialog.tsx:19 shows changedBy/changedAt and a Reload action for 409 VERSION_CONFLICT; apps/erp/src/lib/queries.ts:225-235 toasts describeError on mutation failure; setError mapping of API field errors in 10 feature files`, `No in-app navigation guard: useBlocker/unstable_useBlocker appears nowhere in apps/erp/src; only two wizards register beforeunload; apps/erp/src/components/ui/sheet.tsx and dialog.tsx have no onInteractOutside/onEscapeKeyDown dirty check, so clicking outside a half-filled CustomerFormSheet (CustomerFormSheet.tsx:229) discards it silently; SettingsPage.tsx:213 only shows an 'Unsaved changes' label`, `apps/web/components/forms/EnquiryForm.tsx:52-56 clear copy ('Enter a valid 10-digit Indian mobile number.'), :107 maps server field errors, :119 success card role=status aria-live=polite`

### H4. Empty/loading/error states, console errors, resilience — 760/1000 (v1: 530)

**Strengths.** Every ERP list and detail page has skeleton, empty and error states with a retry, the error state surfaces the server request id for support, and route-level boundaries reset on navigation. The portal is cached-first with offline-aware error copy, both clients avoid retrying 4xx and refresh tokens single-flight across tabs, and the QA runs prove the console stays clean on every page.

**Gaps.** The portal relies on expo-router's default error boundary with no branded fallback, and the website lacks a global-error.tsx and loading.tsx so root-layout failures and slow dynamic renders fall to framework defaults. Caught render errors are never reported anywhere (onError unused, no Sentry), so production crashes are invisible to the team. The ERP retry policy ignores Retry-After on 503 load-shed responses (one immediate retry, no backoff) and has no offline indicator, so staff on flaky site connectivity get generic failures.

**Second auditor.** Verified: apps/erp/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:16-47 and RouteErrorBoundary keyed on pathname; error-state.tsx:18-27 prints describeError plus the API request id and a Retry button; app.tsx:13-18 retries once and never on 4xx; api.ts:93-130 parses Retry-After into the client error but no caller uses it for backoff. grep for ErrorBoundary/componentDidCatch in apps/portal/src → nothing; apps/web/app has (site)/error.tsx, (legal)/error.tsx and not-found.tsx but no global-error.tsx or loading.tsx. ErrorBoundary.onError is never wired to a reporter. On the plus side the portal clears its persisted cache on logout (queries.ts:53-55, AuthProvider.tsx:77) and the API uses @fastify/under-pressure with Retry-After (app.ts:72-80), so overload degrades gracefully server-side. 760 reflects a strong ERP story with real gaps on the two customer-facing surfaces and no crash reporting anywhere.

**World-best would.** Wire boundaries and the API client to an error reporter with request ids, honour Retry-After with jittered backoff on 429/503, add portal and website global boundaries, and show an online/offline status with queued mutations for field staff.

Evidence: `apps/erp/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:16-47 ErrorBoundary with reset, RouteErrorBoundary keyed on pathname mounted in AppShell.tsx:120-124; apps/erp/src/app.tsx:41 Suspense FullPageLoader over 52 lazy routes`, `apps/erp/src/components/ui/error-state.tsx:21,30 shows describeError text, the API request_id and a Retry button; empty-state.tsx; Skeleton in 49, ErrorState in 40, EmptyState in 26 of 135 feature files; data-table.tsx:271,347 inline error state and refreshing progressbar`, `apps/erp/src/app.tsx:13-18 shouldRetry: one retry, never on 4xx; apps/erp/src/lib/api.ts:6,348-450 single-flight refresh with a Web Lock across tabs, Retry-After parsing (:93-157) into user copy`, `apps/portal/src/api/queries.ts:26-40 networkMode offlineFirst, AsyncStorage persister (7 days), refetchOnMount 'always', retry < 2 non-4xx; apps/portal/src/ui/States.tsx:27-46 ErrorState distinguishes offline / not-found / generic with 'Try again'; StatusBar used 21 times for cached/offline strips`, `apps/web/app/(site)/error.tsx:5 and (legal)/error.tsx route error pages, app/not-found.tsx branded 404; ClickToLoadEmbed.tsx poster fallback onError (docs/qa/web.md defect 7)`, `docs/qa/erp.md:10, web.md:12, portal.md:5 — zero console errors and zero failed requests across 622 audited pages; gaps: no ErrorBoundary anywhere in apps/portal/src (framework default only), no apps/web/app/global-error.tsx or loading.tsx, ErrorBoundary.onError is never wired to any reporter, ERP has no navigator.onLine/offline banner`

### H5. Indian localisation: INR grouping and short forms, amount in words, IST dates, FY numbering — 750/1000 (v1: 730)

**Strengths.** The domain package gets the hard parts right and tests them: paise integers, Indian grouping, Cr/L short forms, Indian-system words, April–March financial-year numbering with configurable prefixes, and IST-scheduled jobs. Receipts, demand letters, allotment letters and statements all carry amount-in-words, and every client formats with en-IN and Asia/Kolkata.

**Gaps.** No rupee-capable font is bundled, so production PDFs fall back to 'Rs.' rather than ₹ on company letterhead. The same formatting logic is re-implemented in the ERP, portal and website instead of being imported from @kkt/domain, with almost no client tests, so rounding or grouping drift between a receipt PDF and the screen is possible. Business instants shown with formatDate in the ERP use the browser's zone instead of IST, and phone numbers are never formatted for display. There is no Hindi or regional-language support anywhere.

**Second auditor.** Domain side verified and tested: packages/domain/src/money.ts exports groupIndian (:156), formatINR (:178), formatINRShort (:206), integerToIndianWords (:259), amountInWords (:289); fy.ts:38-53 yields '2526' labels; numbering.ts:18-24 allocates {prefix}{fy}/{seq:4} per company and FY; 124 domain tests pass (ran here). The PDF rupee claim is confirmed and slightly worse than stated: apps/api/assets/fonts does not exist at all (ls fails), so findRupeeFont() returns null and PDF_CURRENCY_PREFIX is 'Rs. ' (pdf.ts:66-91) on every receipt, demand letter, allotment letter and statement a customer receives. ERP formatDate (format.ts:218-221) uses date-fns in browser-local time and is applied to instants at CustomerDetailPage.tsx:345, RateCardsOverviewPage.tsx:117, RateCardsTab.tsx:145-146, CustomerStatementPage.tsx:202 (OtherChargesTab slices to a date first). Four parallel formatter implementations with 7 ERP tests and 0 web/portal tests. A builder's customer-facing documents missing the ₹ symbol is a visible defect, hence 750.

**World-best would.** Bundle a Noto Sans/Devanagari TTF with the ₹ glyph for PDFs, expose one browser-safe `@kkt/domain/format` entry consumed by all clients with snapshot tests of the ₹1 Cr worked example, format every instant in IST, and add phone formatting plus an i18n layer for Hindi.

Evidence: `packages/domain/src/money.ts:156 groupIndian, :178 formatINR, :206 formatINRShort, :259-289 integerToIndianWords/amountInWords ('Rupees Twelve Lakh Fifty Thousand Only'); packages/domain/src/fy.ts:38-53 financialYear → label '2526' for Apr-2025..Mar-2026; 124 domain tests pass (ran: 11 files, 124 tests)`, `apps/api/src/lib/numbering.ts:18-24 per-company, per-FY sequences with pattern {prefix}{fy}/{seq:4} → CUST-2526/0001, BK-2526/0001, SGB/2526/0001; apps/api/src/jobs/index.ts:28-33 all cron schedules tz Asia/Kolkata (dues 00:30, integrity 00:45)`, `apps/erp/src/lib/format.ts:8-20 Indian 3-2-2 grouping, :72-88 ₹1.2 Cr / ₹45 L short forms, :168-195 amountInWords, :213-245 IST formatDateTime via Intl en-IN; MoneyInput parses '45L'/'1.2 Cr' (format.ts:108-140); apps/web/lib/format.ts:5-132 and apps/portal/src/lib/format.ts:4-83 Intl en-IN with timeZone IST`, `apps/api/src/modules/reports/services/documents.ts:61,97,165,252 — receipts, demand letters, allotment letters and statements print amount in words; pdf.ts:12 imports formatINR/amountInWords/istParts from @kkt/domain`, `apps/api/src/modules/reports/services/pdf.ts:66-71,88-91 — PDF_CURRENCY_PREFIX is '₹' only if a TTF exists under apps/api/assets/fonts; that directory is empty (ls apps/api/assets → no files), so every generated PDF prints 'Rs. 12,50,000' instead of ₹`, `Four independent formatter implementations (domain, erp, portal, web) with only 7 ERP format tests (apps/erp/src/lib/__tests__/format.test.ts) and none in web/portal; apps/erp/src/lib/format.ts:218-221 formatDate parses instants with date-fns in browser-local time (6 call sites on *_at fields, e.g. CustomerDetailPage.tsx:345) rather than IST; docs/qa/web.md:62 phones rendered raw ('6122200000')`

### H6. Observability: structured logs, request ids, metrics, health/readiness, tracing, alerting hooks — 600/1000 (v1: 490)

**Strengths.** The basics the spec asks for are present and correct: structured pino JSON with secret redaction, request ids that are generated or propagated and echoed in headers, error bodies and audit rows, liveness and readiness probes that really test Postgres and Redis, a Prometheus-format endpoint, and a durable job_runs table for every scheduled job.

**Gaps.** The metrics endpoint is effectively unscrapable (staff JWT required, per-worker counters under node:cluster) and omits the signals an operator needs — pool saturation, Redis latency, queue depth, job duration, payment/dues counters. Business code barely logs, so debugging a wrong demand or a failed nightly job relies on the audit table alone. Job failures are swallowed into a FAILED row with no retry, no alert and no page; there is no tracing across API → Postgres → Redis → worker, no error reporter, no alert routing, and the worker process has no health check.

**Second auditor.** All cited facts check out and I found one more gap. logger.ts:5-8 pino with redaction; requestContext.ts:6-16 X-Request-Id/X-Response-Time, genReqId honours a sane inbound id; errors.ts:63,85 envelope carries request_id; system/index.ts:23-48 real DB+Redis probes for /health and /ready (503 + Retry-After). But /metrics is registered with permission 'admin.tools' (system/index.ts:51) and counters are per process (metrics.ts:1, app.ts:104) under node:cluster, so Prometheus cannot scrape it unattended and sees one random worker. runJob (jobs/index.ts:55-68) catches every error and returns FAILED, so the BullMQ 'failed' handler (worker.ts:43) and any retry never fire. Business modules emit 4 log lines in total (grep across apps/api/src/modules). The extra gap: although GET /admin/jobs and /admin/system exist (tools.ts:197,260) and count FAILED runs in the last 24 h (tools.ts:130), the ERP has no page that calls them (grep '/admin/jobs' in apps/erp/src → none; apps/erp/src/features/admin has no jobs/system page), so a failed nightly DAILY_DUES run is invisible to every staff user without API access. No OpenTelemetry, Sentry, alert webhook or worker health endpoint anywhere.

**World-best would.** Adopt OpenTelemetry (traces, metrics, logs) exported to a collector, expose /metrics on an internal port or bearer scrape token with aggregated multi-process metrics, instrument domain events and job outcomes, let failures propagate to BullMQ retries with alerting on failed runs, and ship Grafana dashboards plus SLO alerts with the compose stack.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/lib/logger.ts:5-8 pino with level from LOG_LEVEL and path redaction of authorization/cookie/password/token; apps/api/src/plugins/requestContext.ts:6-16 X-Request-Id + X-Response-Time on every reply, :19-24 genReqId honours a sane inbound X-Request-Id; apps/api/src/lib/errors.ts:63,85-86 error envelope carries request_id and logs unhandled errors; apps/api/src/lib/audit.ts:63,75 audit rows store requestId`, `apps/api/src/modules/system/index.ts:23-41 /health pings DB and Redis (status ok|degraded), /ready answers 503 with Retry-After when either is down; apps/api/src/lib/metrics.ts:11-20 http_requests_total + http_request_duration_seconds histogram + uptime/heap gauges in Prometheus text format; apps/api/src/jobs/index.ts:54-70 every job run persisted in job_runs with status/result/error`, `apps/api/src/modules/system/index.ts:51 — /metrics is registered with permission 'admin.tools', i.e. needs a 15-minute staff JWT; there is no scrape token, so Prometheus cannot collect it unattended`, `apps/api/src/lib/metrics.ts:1 comment and server.ts:62-80 — counters are per process while API_WORKERS forks N workers, so a scrape hits one random worker; no DB-pool, Redis, BullMQ queue-depth or job-duration metrics are exported`, `grep over apps/api/src/modules: 1 req.log and 3 logger.* calls in all business modules — payments posted, stages jumped, dues raised and integrity findings produce no operational log lines beyond Fastify's per-request record; apps/api/src/jobs/index.ts:63-68 runJob catches and returns FAILED, so BullMQ's failed handler (worker.ts:43) and retries never fire`, `No OpenTelemetry/tracing, Sentry or alert webhook anywhere (grep opentelemetry|otel|sentry|alert → none); worker.ts exposes no health endpoint; docker-compose.yml ships no log shipper, Prometheus or Grafana`

### H7. Deployment architecture — 580/1000 (v1: 380)

**Strengths.** The three-domain, one-backend topology is implemented end to end with a working Compose stack: Caddy terminates TLS and routes /api on every host to pooled API replicas, the ERP and portal are static nginx images, the website is a standalone Next server, the worker is a separate process, and every environment variable is documented and validated at boot. There is no vendor lock-in — the previous implementation's Emergent dependency is gone.

**Gaps.** This is a single-VM recipe, not a production architecture: file storage is a local volume (the S3 driver is a throw), so it cannot scale past one host or survive host loss; production Compose seeds demo data by default; the PgBouncer/partitioning/restore artefacts the docs promise are missing; API_WORKERS is left at 1; website image optimisation silently degrades because build args are not passed. There is no infrastructure-as-code for a cloud deployment, no automated backups, no secrets management, and CI neither builds the portal nor runs the browser QA that the quality bar depends on.

**Second auditor.** Confirmed each cited defect and found two more. docker-compose.yml:35 `SEED_ON_BOOT: ${SEED_ON_BOOT:-true}` under NODE_ENV=production and config.ts:28 defaults to true, with server.ts:64-66 seeding whenever the demo company has no projects; storage.ts:34 throws on STORAGE_DRIVER=s3 while config.ts:23-25 and .env.example:20-26 parse S3_* variables that nothing uses; packages/db/partitions, docker-compose.prod.yml, restore.md all absent; API_WORKERS not set in compose (default 1, config.ts:36) although DEPLOY.md:53 requires auto; the web service receives NEXT_PUBLIC_* as runtime environment while apps/web/Dockerfile:6-12 expects build ARGs, so images.remotePatterns bakes localhost and SmartImage.tsx:64-70 falls back to unoptimized <img> (server-side URLs are read dynamically via lib/env.ts, so links do work). New: apps/erp/nginx.conf and apps/portal/nginx.conf set no security headers and the Caddyfile adds only HSTS/nosniff/Referrer-Policy, so the staff ERP and portal are served with no Content-Security-Policy and no X-Frame-Options/frame-ancestors (helmet runs only on the API, CSP disabled at app.ts:63; next.config.ts sets DENY for the website only) while the ERP keeps its refresh token in localStorage (api.ts:5,12). The worker service has no healthcheck, and the API image copies the whole /repo/node_modules including dev dependencies (Dockerfile:24). CI builds api/erp/web but never the portal and runs no browser QA or load test. Zero Emergent references — no lock-in. A working single-VM recipe, not a production architecture: 580.

**World-best would.** Ship the S3/GCS adapter and CDN-fronted media, a Terraform/Helm reference deployment (managed Postgres with PgBouncer and replicas, managed Redis, object storage, autoscaled API/worker), default SEED_ON_BOOT=false in production with an explicit demo flag, automated pg_dump + restore drills, and CI that builds all four artefacts and runs the Playwright QA.

Evidence: `docker-compose.yml:1-95 — postgres (tuned infra/postgres/postgresql.conf), redis, api (replicas 2, healthcheck), separate worker, erp and portal as nginx static images, web as Next standalone, caddy; infra/caddy/Caddyfile:5-22 erp./www./my. hosts, /api/* reverse-proxied with least_conn + health_uri, HSTS/nosniff; apex redirects to www`, `apps/api/Dockerfile:1-27 multi-stage pnpm build, non-root kkt user, migrations copied by tsup; apps/erp/Dockerfile + nginx.conf (immutable /assets, SPA fallback); apps/web/Dockerfile standalone output; apps/portal/Dockerfile expo export; .env.example documents every variable incl. API_WORKERS/DATABASE_POOL_MAX/API_MAX_INFLIGHT; apps/api/src/config.ts:13-15 refuses short secrets; grep 'emergent' across v2 code → none (no platform lock-in)`, `docker-compose.yml:35 SEED_ON_BOOT: ${SEED_ON_BOOT:-true} under NODE_ENV=production — a fresh production database is seeded with the Shree Ganesh demo (12 customers, Welcome@123) unless the operator remembers to override; apps/api/src/server.ts:64-66 seeds whenever the demo company has no projects`, `apps/api/src/lib/storage.ts:33-34 — STORAGE_DRIVER=s3 throws 'S3 storage driver not bundled'; uploads live on a single Docker volume (storage), so the api replicas and worker only work on one host and there is no object-storage/CDN path despite docs/DEPLOY.md:34`, `Documented infrastructure that does not exist: docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:103 packages/db/partitions, :104 docker-compose.prod.yml with PgBouncer, docs/DEPLOY.md:54 restore.md — none present; compose does not set API_WORKERS=auto although DEPLOY.md requires it; web service passes NEXT_PUBLIC_* as runtime env but next.config.ts:8-23 bakes images.remotePatterns at build time, so production images bypass the optimizer (SmartImage.tsx:64-70 falls back to unoptimized)`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml:29-36 builds api/erp/web but never exports the portal, runs no browser QA or load test; no Terraform/Kubernetes/Helm, no backup cron, no secrets manager, no CDN or WAF configuration`

### H8. Seed/demo data coherence — 850/1000 (v1: 700)

**Strengths.** The demo is built through the production code paths, self-validates the spec's hardest invariants (zero integrity findings, the ₹1 Cr ledger to the paise, 52-flat tower, portal isolation) and is re-verified by an integration test that passed in this audit. Reseed is triple-guarded, restores identical numbering and preserves the audit trail, and every spec-listed scenario record exists and is interconnected.

**Gaps.** The demo still reads as a demo in places: no floor plans, placeholder video/tour/map ids and text hero images, staff without phones so the portal's call/WhatsApp buttons never render, and a notification backlog for Accounts. SEED_ON_BOOT defaults to true even in the production Compose file, so the guard that protects reseed does not protect first boot. Seeding takes ~23 s per run which slows every acceptance file, and the documented numbers (BK-2526/…) differ from what a reader sees after April 2026 because dates are relative.

**Second auditor.** The strongest factor in the group and the claims verify. validate.ts:26-58 asserts zero integrity findings, the ten SPEC §11 ledger rows bucket/amount/cash/account/total to the paise, booking #1 COMPLETED with zero due and P1 SOLD, cancelled B-303 released, Tower C = 52 flats with no 13xx/7xx, and per-customer portal isolation by logging in as each customer. Reseed is guarded three ways: AdminReseedRequestSchema requires confirm literal 'RESEED' (contracts/src/modules/admin.ts:32), the route requires Super Admin and throws RESEED_DISABLED in production (tools.ts:267-271), and run.ts:131 re-checks NODE_ENV. docs/SEED.md:92-97 is candid about relative dates and the 2526/2627 prefix drift. The residual placeholders (no FLOOR_PLAN docs, staff without phones, fake video/map ids, 63 unread bells) are documented in docs/qa/portal.md and web.md. The only thing the seed does not protect against is first boot in production (SEED_ON_BOOT default), which I count under deployment; 850.

**World-best would.** Ship real floor-plan renders, hero photography and working media ids, give staff phones so every portal feature demos, default SEED_ON_BOOT off outside development, and snapshot the seeded database so tests and demos load in seconds.

Evidence: `apps/api/src/seed/run.ts:68-100 seedDemo drives the real API in-process (Idempotency-Key, If-Match, role-specific users) and skips when projects exist; apps/api/src/seed/fallbacks.ts:2-5 documents the only two direct-SQL exceptions (back-dated brochure downloads, past site visits)`, `apps/api/src/seed/validate.ts:26 asserts integrity ok with zero findings, :32-42 the ten SPEC §11 ledger rows to the paise + COMPLETED/SOLD, :44 cancelled flat released, :48-49 Tower C = 52 flats with no 13xx/7xx, :52-58 every portal login sees exactly its own bookings, :86-87 exact counts (12 customers, 8 leads, 10 bookings, 10 visits, 5 tickets, 2 notices, 4+1 posts, 130 flats, 6 testimonials, 18 gallery…)`, `apps/api/test/integration/seed.test.ts:77-209 — 8 tests ran green here in 72 s on kkt_audit_h: validation, integrity zero, portal isolation + masking, public site view, idempotent second run, reseed() restores identical counts and receipt numbers while audit_logs grow, POST /admin/reseed`, `Reseed guard: apps/api/src/modules/admin/tools.ts:267-275 permission admin.tools + body confirm 'RESEED' + 422 RESEED_DISABLED in production; apps/api/src/seed/run.ts:130-131 throws when NODE_ENV=production; truncates business tables incl. sequences and idempotency keys, keeps companies/roles/users/audit_logs, writes an admin.reseed audit row (docs/SEED.md:92-93)`, `docs/SEED.md:52-66 — ten bookings cover every spec scenario (worked example, pending cheque, bounced cheque, reversal, revision, cancellation with refund, Flexi time trigger, co-applicant + parking + shared docs); testimonials tied to seeded customers; construction photos per completed stage`, `Residual placeholders from docs/qa/portal.md:78-80 and web.md:60: no FLOOR_PLAN documents (My Flat always shows a placeholder), staff users have no phone (RM call/WhatsApp untestable), placeholder YouTube/Matterport/Maps ids, text-only hero images, Accounts user starts with 63 unread bells; docs/SEED.md:99 RESOLVED ticket left unrated by rule`

### H9. Documentation quality & honesty of claims — 760/1000 (v1: 510)

**Strengths.** The documentation set is unusually complete and candid: stack decisions carry reasons, every API surface is pinned in appendices, the acceptance doc maps each spec scenario to a test file with notes, and the performance and status documents say plainly where targets were missed and what remains open. The QA reports record defects found and the gaps they did not cover.

**Gaps.** Several forward-looking infrastructure claims are stated as fact but have no artefact behind them (partition scripts, PgBouncer compose file, restore guide, S3 configuration), and the README overstates what CI runs and what 'lint' means. The audit file the README and STATUS link to does not exist yet. There are no architecture decision records, changelog, operational runbook or incident playbook, which a builder's IT team would need on day one.

**Second auditor.** The documentation is broad and the performance/status docs are genuinely honest (PERFORMANCE.md:44-50 'not met on this box', bookings page 404 ms; STATUS.md:30-37 open items). But the number of phantom artefacts is higher than a 790 suggests: ARCHITECTURE.md:103-104 describe partitioning scripts in packages/db/partitions and PgBouncer in docker-compose.prod.yml as existing (neither exists); DEPLOY.md:34 tells operators to set STORAGE_DRIVER=s3 which makes the API refuse to boot (storage.ts:34); DEPLOY.md:54 points to a restore.md that is not in the repo; README.md:20 and STATUS.md:27 link docs/AUDIT-80-FACTORS-V2.md which is absent; README.md:33 says CI runs 'all of them' including pnpm loadtest, which ci.yml:29-36 does not, nor the portal build or browser QA; STATUS.md:21 says 30 integration files, there are 31 .test.ts; 'lint' is tsc --noEmit in api/erp/web (package.json) with only the portal using expo lint; .env.example documents S3_* variables that are dead. No ADRs, CHANGELOG or runbooks. Candid where it counts, but a builder's IT team following DEPLOY.md would hit three dead ends on day one: 760.

**World-best would.** Keep a doc-to-artefact check in CI (every path mentioned in docs must exist), add ADRs and a CHANGELOG, write runbooks for backup/restore, key rotation, reseed and incident response, and publish the OpenAPI document and QA reports as versioned release artefacts.

Evidence: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md (stack with reasons, module map, phase plan), docs/DATA-MODEL.md (77 tables described; grep pgTable = 77 in packages/db/src/schema), docs/API-CONVENTIONS.md (envelopes, headers, fixed Appendix A/B/C contracts), docs/SECURITY.md, docs/ACCEPTANCE.md (scenario → test file → notes, plus defects found while writing), docs/SEED.md, per-app READMEs of 81–112 lines`, `docs/PERFORMANCE.md:40-60 honest assessment ('not met on this box' for 1,000 connections, bookings page 404 ms at 200), raw before/after tables; docs/STATUS.md:30-37 'Known open items (honest list)'; docs/TODO-integration.md marks items 6, 7, 10 as deferred; docs/qa/*.md list every defect fixed and what was not covered (lockout, MFA, erasure, contrast)`, `Claims that do not match the repo: docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:103 packages/db/partitions and :104 docker-compose.prod.yml (PgBouncer) do not exist; docs/DEPLOY.md:54 'included restore.md' is absent; docs/DEPLOY.md:34 instructs STORAGE_DRIVER=s3 while apps/api/src/lib/storage.ts:34 throws; README.md:20 and docs/STATUS.md:27 link docs/AUDIT-80-FACTORS-V2.md which is not present`, `README.md:33 'CI runs all of them on every push' — .github/workflows/ci.yml runs typecheck/unit/integration/acceptance and three builds but not pnpm loadtest, the portal export or the browser QA; lint scripts are tsc --noEmit (apps/*/package.json) with no ESLint config; docs/STATUS.md:21 says 30 integration files, the folder holds 31`, `apps/erp/src/styles/index.css:4 cites a 'legacy design_guidelines.json' that is not in this repository; docs/SEED.md:95 explains the BK-2526 vs BK-2627 numbering drift; no ADRs, CHANGELOG, runbook or on-call/incident guide`, `docs/DEPLOY.md:34 'set STORAGE_DRIVER=s3 with bucket/credentials' vs apps/api/src/lib/storage.ts:34 throw new Error("S3 storage driver not bundled in this build…")`

### H10. Overall production readiness for a real builder today — 650/1000 (v1: 430)

**Strengths.** An auditor would find a rebuilt system whose money, security and concurrency rules are encoded as pure engines with exhaustive tests, verified end-to-end by acceptance scenarios and seed validation, and whose UI was mechanically checked on phone and desktop with zero console errors. The documentation is honest about limits, and the single-VM Compose stack would run a small or mid-size builder's pilot today.

**Gaps.** Before go-live for a large builder the auditor would require: durable object storage and a multi-node deployment actually tested, production defaults that cannot seed demo data, scrapable metrics with alerting and tracing so a failed nightly dues run pages someone, automated backups with a rehearsed restore, a rupee font in PDFs, AA contrast fixes, a resolution of the COMPLETED-booking reversal rule, native app builds through app-store review, browser coverage of lockout/MFA/erasure, and an external penetration test. The 1,000-concurrent-staff target rests on an untested horizontal-scaling argument.

**Second auditor.** The verification base is real: I ran domain (124/124) and contracts (182/182) tests and `pnpm typecheck` (7/7 green, turbo-cached from an identical tree), and the first auditor ran seed/system integration files green. Core money/security engineering is credible. But the go-live list is longer than the first auditor's: (1) a fresh production stack seeds twelve demo customers with password Welcome@123 and six staff logins unless SEED_ON_BOOT is overridden (docker-compose.yml:35, config.ts:28, server.ts:64-66); (2) object storage is a throw, so two API replicas and the worker share one Docker volume and the system cannot survive host loss or scale out (storage.ts:34); (3) no automated backup/restore path beyond a Super-Admin zip (backup.ts:395) and a missing restore.md; (4) job failures never retry or alert and the ERP has no page showing job health, so a failed nightly dues run would go unnoticed; (5) metrics are unscrapable; (6) the staff ERP is served without CSP or frame-ancestors while keeping refresh tokens in localStorage; (7) customer PDFs print 'Rs.'; (8) AA contrast fails at the token level; (9) CI never builds the portal and native builds were never run; (10) COMPLETED-booking reversal semantics remain an open owner decision (TODO-integration.md:7). Horizontal scaling to the 5-lakh public target is argued, not demonstrated. A pilot-ready system for a mid-size builder; a large builder's auditor would block go-live until items 1-6 are closed: 650.

**World-best would.** Treat the above as a go-live checklist with owners and dates, run a staged pilot on cloud infrastructure with monitoring, backups and chaos/failover drills, commission a third-party security and accessibility audit, and keep the acceptance, browser QA and load tests running in CI against every release candidate.

Evidence: `Verification base is real: pnpm typecheck green for 7 packages (turbo, this run), domain 124/124 and ERP 94/94 unit tests ran green here, system.test.ts 6/6 and seed.test.ts 8/8 integration tests ran green on a fresh database; docs/ACCEPTANCE.md maps 13 of 14 spec scenarios to 55 passing tests; docs/qa/*.md 622 clean page audits at 1440/390 px`, `Core correctness and security are production-grade: append-only audit trigger, two-bucket ledger under advisory locks, idempotent payments, If-Match versions, EdDSA JWT audiences, field encryption, scope → 404 (docs/SECURITY.md, docs/ARCHITECTURE.md §3-5, acceptance scenarios 3-6, 10)`, `Go-live blockers in code: apps/api/src/lib/storage.ts:34 no object storage (single-host volume); docker-compose.yml:35 production seeds demo data by default; apps/api/src/modules/system/index.ts:51 metrics unscrapable and no tracing/alerting (apps/api/src/jobs/index.ts:63-68 job failures never alert); no automated backups or restore procedure (docs/DEPLOY.md:54 points to a missing file); apps/api/assets/fonts empty so PDFs print 'Rs.'`, `Capacity: docs/PERFORMANCE.md:40-46 — 300 ms p95 met for lists at 200 connections but not at 1,000 zero-think-time connections on one 4-vCPU box; horizontal scaling is asserted (stateless workers, Redis, replica handle) but never exercised on more than one node, and the bookings list is 404 ms at 200`, `Coverage holes acknowledged in docs/STATUS.md:30-37 and docs/qa/erp.md:67: native iOS/Android builds never run, lockout/MFA/erasure untested in the browser, WCAG contrast unmeasured (and failing per token math), no ESLint, zero unit tests in apps/web and apps/portal, CI does not build the portal`, `Open product decisions: docs/TODO-integration.md items 6-7 (reversal on a COMPLETED booking leaves it COMPLETED with due > 0; other-charge notification keying) and 10 (Activity report link not gated on audit.view) remain deferred`
